Evaluations across literary, financial, and legal domains demonstrate that RH-RAG consistently improves factual grounding, semantic coherence, and document-level alignment compared to standard and hierarchical RAG baselines, while achieving reliability competitive with proprietary cloud-based systems without compromising data privacy.

</details>


### [170] [BiCAA: Bidirectional Credit Assignment for Search-Augmented Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01321)
*Yibin Huang,Bin Xu,Hailong Cao,Conghui Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCAA框架，通过双向信用分配将过程奖励引入多步搜索代理的训练，解决vanilla GRPO仅基于最终结果进行稀疏监督导致的训练不稳定和冗余搜索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步搜索是搜索代理的关键能力，但vanilla GRPO仅基于最终输出分配奖励，缺乏对中间推理步骤的监督信号，导致训练不稳定和冗余搜索行为。

Method: BiCAA构建双向过程奖励，融合两个互补信号：前向可解性增益（量化每步答案似然性改进）和事后成功关键性（基于结果的回溯关键性评分评估每步对最终成功的必要性），将两者调制聚合后与结果奖励融合。

Result: 在搜索增强QA基准上的实验表明，BiCAA能够稳定策略优化、减少冗余搜索行为，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过双向信用分配提供稠密且有区分度的过程奖励，能有效缓解稀疏监督带来的训练问题，提升多步搜索代理的学习效果。

Abstract: Multi-step search is a fundamental capability for search agents, enabling them to iteratively acquire, refine, and integrate external evidence for complex reasoning QA. However, vanilla GRPO allocates rewards exclusively based on the model's final outputs, yielding outcome-only supervision with no supervisory signals for intermediate reasoning steps. Such sparse supervision easily causes training instability and redundant search behaviors on multi-step search tasks. To mitigate this limitation, we adopt process reward to deliver stepwise supervision signals. For this process reward, we propose two complementary criteria to judge each search step: whether the step yields new evidence to facilitate problem solving, and whether it forms an efficient, pivotal intermediate decision within the overall reasoning trajectory. Building on this insight, we propose BiCAA: a bidirectional credit assignment framework that delivers dense, distinguishing process rewards for search-augmented agents. BiCAA builds bidirectional process rewards by fusing two complementary signals: forward solvability gain and hindsight success criticality. The former quantifies step-wise improvements in answer plausibility, while the latter evaluates each step's necessity for final success via hindsight outcome-based criticality scoring. We modulate and aggregate the two signals and then fuse them with the outcome reward. Experiments on search-augmented QA benchmarks show that BiCAA stabilizes policy optimization, reduces redundant search behavior, and achieves competitive performance.

</details>


### [171] [Can Language Models Identify Shadow Trading Targets? An NLP Evaluation of SEC Enforcement Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01322)
*Sarah Wilson,Michael MacKay,Anthony Marello,Trinav Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用NLP（两阶段LLM流水线）分析SEC 10-K文件的Item 7部分，以在事前识别"影子交易"中的经济关联同行公司。虽然该流水线能识别已知案例中的对应公司，但在217个同行观测数据的统计检验中，语义相似度与公告日异常股票回报之间没有显著关联（秩相关系数+0.07，p=0.37），对影子交易执法的实证前提提出了质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影子交易是一种新颖且有争议的内幕交易责任理论，源于SEC v. Panuwat案。执法需要在事前识别经济关联公司，但SEC仅依赖事后的大规模市场监控基础设施。作者探究NLP能否履行SEC理论所假设的"内部人已知"的同行识别功能，从而检验影子交易执法的实证基础。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM流水线，分析五个行业的30起M&A事件中SEC 10-K文件的Item 7（管理层讨论与分析）部分，计算语义相似度，并将其与公告日异常股票回报相关联。使用排列检验和Spearman相关系数进行统计分析，并在Panuwat案事实模式上进行稳健性检查。

Result: 在Panuwat案的模式中，流水线成功将Incyte识别为最接近的同行之一（已知结果案例的合理性检验通过）。但全数据集显示无关联：217个同行观测的组内秩相关为+0.07（排列p=0.37），每事件平均Spearman相关为+0.05，95%置信区间[-0.08, +0.18]，排除了中等程度关系的存在。30个事件中14个支持假设，12个矛盾，4个模糊。此外，Incyte在公告前一天落在标准的20亿至100亿美元中盘股区间之外。

Conclusion: 研究结果对影子交易执法的实证前提构成压力，并涉及SEC金融监控基础设施相关的宪法问题。结果为探索性的，受限于该流水线、语料库和回报度量方式，但为同行识别方法提供了重要的实证检验视角。

Abstract: Shadow trading -- trading in a peer firm's securities on the basis of material nonpublic information (MNPI) about an "economically linked" company -- is a novel and contested theory of insider trading liability, first prosecuted in SEC v. Panuwat (2023). Enforcing it requires identifying economically linked firms ex ante, a determination the SEC makes only after the fact using mass market surveillance infrastructure. We ask whether NLP can do what the SEC's theory presumes insiders already know: identify peer firms ex ante from publicly mandated disclosures. Using a two-stage LLM pipeline applied to Item 7 (Management's Discussion and Analysis) sections of SEC 10-K filings, we score semantic similarity across 30 M&A events spanning five industries and relate similarity to announcement-day abnormal stock returns. On the Panuwat fact pattern itself the pipeline recovers Incyte among the closest peers, a sanity check on the one case with a known outcome. Across the full dataset, however, we find no association: pooling 217 peer observations, the within-event rank correlation between similarity and abnormal return is +0.07 (permutation p = 0.37), and the mean per-event Spearman correlation is +0.05 with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.08, +0.18] -- narrow enough to exclude any moderate relationship rather than merely failing to detect one. A case-level reading agrees: 14 of 30 events support the hypothesis, 12 contradict it, and 4 are ambiguous. We also find that Incyte fell outside the standard \$2B-\$10B mid-cap band on the day before the announcement, complicating the "mid-cap oncology" category the SEC invoked. These results are exploratory and bound to this pipeline, corpus, and return measure, but they put pressure on the empirical premise of shadow trading enforcement and bear on constitutional questions surrounding the SEC's financial surveillance infrastructure.

</details>


### [172] [LongChart VQA: A Comprehensive Benchmark for MLLMs with Complex Multi-Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01328)
*Ziyan Xiao,Yinghao Zhu,Wenting Zhang,Heaju Kim,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LongChart基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在多图表理解和多步推理方面的能力。通过基于潜在图的合成流程构建包含平均6.5张图像和31.2个问题的VQA数据集，评估了10个先进MLLM在推理模式、辅助工具和图像扰动鲁棒性三个维度上的表现，发现准确率随计算复杂度增加而下降且波动较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要强调单图表感知能力，简单的图表间连接不足以评估MLLM在多图表理解、多步推理等复杂智能体任务中的能力，因此需要更全面的多图表基准。

Method: 设计基于潜在图支持的合成流程，构建LongChart基准数据集，包含平均6.5张图像和31.2个问题的VQA集合，评估10个先进MLLM，并考察推理模式、辅助工具和图像扰动鲁棒性三个影响因素。

Result: 研究发现MLLM的准确率随着计算复杂度的增加而下降，且性能波动明显，揭示了多图表推理领域需要进一步研究的方向。

Conclusion: 多图表推理仍是MLLM的挑战性任务，当前模型在该场景下的表现不理想，未来需要在多图表推理方面开展更多研究工作。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving with expanded context windows and stronger reasoning capabilities, enabling multi-chart understanding and multi-step inference. These abilities are increasingly important as MLLMs are adopted in complex agentic tasks. However, existing benchmarks largely emphasize single-chart perception, while simple chart-to-chart connections are insufficient to evaluate these capabilities. To capture multi-chart complexity while ensuring consistency and validity, we design a synthesis pipeline supported by latent graphs. Building on this pipeline, we introduce LongChart, a benchmark whose VQA sets contain an average of 6.5 images and 31.2 questions. We evaluate 10 state-of-the-art MLLMs and examine three factors that influence performance: reasoning patterns, auxiliary tools, and robustness to image perturbations. Our results show that MLLM accuracy decreases and varies substantially as computational complexity increases, highlighting directions for future research in multi-chart reasoning.

</details>


### [173] [Prompt-Induced Waste in Large Reasoning Models: A Preregistered Two-Harness Benchmark of Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01347)
*Sarel Weinberger,Amir Hozez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册基准测试，系统测量了提示词表述对大型推理模型（编码代理）推理成本的影响。在6个模型、2种代理框架和24个确定性编码任务中进行了4,643次有效运行，发现提示词表述可导致推理成本成倍增加而正确率无提升，其中"要求模型开发并比较多种方案"的指令最具浪费性（推理token增加2.4-7.4x），"深入思考"类提示增加1.6-2.2x，而限定范围的效率模板则可减半推理成本。代理框架选择的影响更大，同一任务在Claude Code下成本是pi框架的5-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型作为编码代理会产生推理、工具调用和多次代理回合带来的成本，但提示词措辞对这一支出的因果影响从未被系统测量过，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用预注册基准测试方法，涵盖6个大型推理模型、2个真实代理框架和24个带隐藏评估器的确定性编码任务，共进行4,643次有效运行，包括筛选研究、压力测试、保留集、复制研究和跨提供商研究。

Result: 提示词表述可显著影响推理成本而无需提升正确率："要求比较多种方案"增加推理token 2.4-7.4x；"深入思考"提示增加1.6-2.2x；有界效率模板成本中性且可减半推理。框架选择影响更大，同一模型-任务-提示在Claude Code下成本是pi的5-30倍。误导性架构提示比无关文字昂贵得多，提供商侧缓存降低计费成本但不改变行为。在Kimi-K3和Claude Sonnet 5上的复制研究保持了主要效应方向但揭示了模型特定的敏感性。

Conclusion: 提示词措辞和代理框架设计都会实质性影响代理成本，且往往不带来任务成功率的提升，建议采用有界效率模板来降低推理成本。

Abstract: Large reasoning models used as coding agents incur costs from deliberation, tool calls, and repeated agent turns, yet the causal effect of prompt wording on this spend has not been measured systematically. We present a preregistered benchmark across six large reasoning models, two real agent harnesses, and 24 deterministic coding tasks with hidden evaluators. Across 4,643 valid runs, including screening, stress, holdout, replication, and cross-provider studies, we find that prompt formulation can multiply reasoning cost without improving correctness. Asking the model to develop and compare several approaches is the most consistently wasteful instruction, increasing reasoning tokens by 2.4-7.4x across all models. Generic "think deeply" cues also increase deliberation by 1.6-2.2x, while a bounded-efficiency template specifying scope, acceptance criteria, and a stop condition is cost-neutral and can halve reasoning. Harness choice matters even more: identical model-task-prompt triples cost 5-30x more per success under Claude Code than under pi, mainly because of larger static prefixes and more turns. Misleading architectural hints are far costlier than irrelevant prose, and provider-side caching reduces billed cost without changing behavior, so it must not be treated as efficiency. Replications on Kimi-K3 and Claude Sonnet 5 preserve the main effect directions while revealing model-specific sensitivity to thinking and certainty cues. Overall, prompt wording and harness design materially affect agent cost, often with no gain in task success.

</details>


### [174] [HopRefusalBench: Diagnosing Refusal Failures in Search-Augmented Agents for Multi-Hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01358)
*Jianan Xie,Xin Sun,Zhongqi Chen,Xing Zheng,Qiang Liu,Bowen Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HopRefusalBench，首个针对多跳搜索场景下大语言模型智能体拒绝（拒答）能力的受控基准测试，包含889个基于KILT实体路径构建的不可回答问题，并提出了最终结果分类体系和源感知轨迹指标。实验发现，在十个前沿模型中最好的目标感知正确停止率（TCHR）仅为42.9%，揭示多跳搜索中的拒答能力是评估搜索增强智能体可靠性的重要但被忽视的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拒答基准主要暴露单跳查询表面的缺陷，无法揭示只有在有效的中间推理和检索之后才会出现的多跳不可回答场景下的失败模式。

Method: 构建HopRefusalBench基准（889个不可回答问题，交叉三种不可回答原因与三种拓扑结构），提出最终结果分类体系（目标感知拒答、伪拒答、幻觉完成、搜索预算耗尽）和源感知轨迹指标。

Result: 十个前沿模型中最佳TCHR仅42.9%；根节点和中节点问题比终端问题更难；所有模型在错误前提类问题上TCHR最高、在上下文不明确问题上最低；但当混合类别时，84.7%-98.4%的显式拒答式响应能识别正确原因，瓶颈主要在于难以承诺适当的非答案回应，失败轨迹则发散为幻觉或搜索预算耗尽。

Conclusion: 多跳搜索中的拒答是一个重要的评估问题，该工作为诊断和改进搜索增强智能体的可靠性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Search-augmented large language model agents are increasingly capable of solving knowledge-intensive tasks, but their behavior when a multi-hop question is fundamentally unanswerable remains poorly understood. Existing abstention benchmarks largely expose defects at the surface of single-hop queries and therefore cannot reveal failures that emerge only after valid intermediate reasoning and retrieval. We introduce HopRefusalBench, the first controlled benchmark of refusal within multi-hop search, comprising 889 unanswerable questions constructed from KILT-grounded entity paths. It crosses three causes of unanswerability (answer unknown, false premise, and underspecified context) with root, middle, and terminal topologies, making premise verification, intermediate-bridge validation, and terminal stopping separately observable. We further propose a final-outcome taxonomy spanning target-aware refusal, pseudo-refusal, hallucinated completion, and search-budget exhaustion, together with source-aware trajectory metrics for post-trigger continuation and token waste. Across ten frontier proprietary and open-weight models in search-augmented mode, the best model achieves a target-aware correct halting rate (TCHR) of only 42.9%. Root and middle items are consistently harder than terminal items, and all models attain their highest TCHR on false premises and their lowest on underspecified questions. Yet when pooled across categories, 84.7--98.4% of each model's explicit refusal-like responses identify the correct rationale, localizing the main bottleneck to committing to an appropriate non-answer; failed trajectories instead diverge into hallucination or search-budget exhaustion. These results establish refusal in multi-hop search as a consequential evaluation problem and provide a foundation for diagnosing and improving the reliability of search-augmented agents.

</details>


### [175] [EviSD: Evidence-Conditioned Self-Distillation for Search-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01359)
*Jianan Xie,Xin Sun,Zhongqi Chen,Xing Zheng,Shu Wu,Bowen Song,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EviSD框架，利用实例级支持证据作为特权信息，通过证据条件化自蒸馏来细化搜索增强语言智能体在多轮搜索中的动作级信用分配，在七个问答基准和三个骨干模型上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习只能提供轨迹级信用分配，无法区分多轮搜索过程中单个动作的贡献，导致搜索增强语言智能体的训练效率受限。

Method: EviSD使用实例级支持证据作为搜索动作的特权信息，黄金答案作为答案动作的补充特权信息。训练时学生从原始上下文采样动作，同一模型在动作对齐上下文中作为特权教师重新评分，将分离的教师-学生差距转化为有界校正项应用于结果派生的GRPO优势，且仅作用于生成的动作跨度。

Result: 在七个问答基准和三个不同规模与世代的骨干模型上，EviSD在所有评估设置中均取得最高宏平均精确匹配分数，超过最强对比方法1.3-2.3个百分点，同时仅调整6.7%-15.1%的响应token。

Conclusion: EviSD通过定位特权引导同时保留结果奖励确定的更新方向，无需辅助蒸馏目标或推理时改动，即可有效提升搜索增强语言智能体的问答性能，证明了证据条件化自蒸馏在动作级信用分配中的有效性。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning enables search-augmented language agents to learn from verifiable final answers, but its trajectory-level credit cannot distinguish the contributions of individual actions in a multi-turn search process. We propose EviSD, an evidence-conditioned self-distillation framework that uses instance-level supporting evidence as privileged information for search actions and golden answers as complementary privilege for answer actions. During training, the student samples actions from the original context, while the same model re-scores them as a privileged teacher under an action-aligned context. EviSD converts the detached teacher--student gap into a bounded correction to the outcome-derived GRPO advantage and applies it only to generated action spans. This design localizes privileged guidance while preserving the update direction determined by the outcome reward, without an auxiliary distillation objective or any change at inference time. Across seven question-answering benchmarks and three backbones spanning model scales and generations, EviSD achieves the highest macro-average Exact Match in all evaluated settings, outperforming the strongest compared methods by 1.3--2.3 points while modulating only 6.7%--15.1% of response tokens. Code is available at https://github.com/JiananXie/EviSD.

</details>


### [176] [Language Equality has a Price: A Systematic Investigation of Multi-turn LLM Performance for EU-24+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01395)
*Sherzod Hakimov,Karl Osswald,Jelle Psurek,Eszter Bukovszky,A. Altar Lüser,David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）作为语言代理在30种语言（包括24种欧盟官方语言和6种其他语言）的自博弈目标导向对话游戏中的表现。研究发现商业模型在欧盟所有官方语言中均胜过所有开源模型，且语言覆盖度的提升并未带来服务质量的均等化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估多基于静态任务或偏好判断，缺乏多轮、参考自由的动态评估范式；同时不同语言间的能力差异（尤其是低资源语言）需要被系统量化，以检验语言服务的均等性。

Method: 采用语言无关的目标导向对话游戏框架，通过自博弈方式评估LLM；游戏机制不依赖特定语言，扩展新语言仅需本地化固定的提示词和词汇表文件；在30种语言上对9个开源和商业LLM进行程序化评分。

Result: 没有任何开源模型能很好地覆盖欧盟24种语言；商业模型在所有官方语言上都胜过开源模型。商业系统在公共网页文本少四个数量级的语言中仍保持领先。模型开发地区的语言表现有所提升但不缩小差距。非英语语言相比英语，运行成本平均高出31%，得分平均低10%。

Conclusion: 语言均等化在技术上是可实现的，但不能仅依赖公共网页数据；当前商业模型在跨语言覆盖上明显优于开源模型，然而即使最好的模型也无法完全消除语言间的成本和性能差异，语言服务的均等覆盖仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Abstract: We evaluate large language models (LLMs) as language agents playing goal-directed dialogue games in self-play across 30 languages: the 24 official EU languages plus six others. Unlike static or preference-based evaluation, this paradigm is multi-turn, reference-free and programmatically scored, and because the game mechanics are language-agnostic it extends to a new language by localising a fixed set of prompt and word-list files. Evaluating nine open-weight and commercial LLMs, we find that no open-weight model covers the EU-24 well: in every official language both commercial systems outscore every open-weight model, and the two weakest average below 40 points across the EU-24. The commercial systems stay ahead even in languages with four orders of magnitude less public web text, showing that linguistic parity is achievable, but not from public crawls alone. A model's home region lifts it without closing the gap: Chinese is the strongest of all 30 languages for two Chinese-developed models, yet the best Chinese score of any model belongs to a US commercial system. Coverage is also not parity of service. Pooled over models and languages, the median non-English language costs 31% more to run than English, and scores 10% lower.

</details>


### [177] [When Retrieval Helps and Distracts: Evaluating Evidence-Generating LLMs for Biomedical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01409)
*Pritam Deka,Prabhjot Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生物医学事实验证系统中的证据生成问题，在CARE-XAI基准上比较了多种模型，发现微调LLM在证据生成上最强、生物医学分类器在纯判定上最强，并提出了新的评估指标Bio-GRACE来诊断检索证据的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学事实验证系统不应仅预测主张是支持、反驳还是未涉及，还应生成忠实、完整且对验证有用的证据。现有研究缺乏对证据生成设置的系统性评估。

Method: 在CARE-XAI统一基准（涵盖五个生物医学和健康事实验证来源）上，通过共享评估协议比较了基础指令LLM、PubMed检索增强LLM、微调LLM、仅标签LLM和生物医学编码器分类器。并引入Bio-GRACE（金标准归一化诊断指标）来衡量检索证据是否恢复了参考证据的决策收益。

Result: 生物医学分类器在仅判定预测上最强；微调LLM是证据生成最强的系统。PubMed检索效果参差不齐：对PubMedQA和SciFact等PubMed对齐来源有帮助，但可能分散对更广泛公共卫生主张的注意力。Bio-GRACE显示检索效用因来源而异，并揭示了检索召回率和词汇证据重叠不足以衡量生物医学事实验证。

Conclusion: 研究支持选择性检索策略的重要性，表明检索效用是来源依赖的，需要比传统检索指标更精细的评估方法来理解和改进生物医学事实验证中的证据生成。

Abstract: Biomedical fact-checking systems must do more than predict whether a claim is supported, contradicted, or unaddressed: they should also produce evidence that is faithful, complete, and useful for verification. We study this evidence-generation setting on CARE-XAI, a unified benchmark spanning five biomedical and health fact-checking sources. We compare base instruction LLMs, PubMed retrieval-augmented LLMs, fine-tuned LLMs, label-only LLMs, and biomedical encoder classifiers under a shared evaluation protocol. Biomedical classifiers remain strongest for verdict-only prediction, while fine-tuned LLMs are the strongest evidence-generating systems. PubMed retrieval is mixed: it helps PubMed-aligned sources such as PubMedQA and SciFact, but can distract models on broader public-health claims. We introduce Bio-GRACE, a gold-reference-normalized diagnostic for measuring whether retrieved evidence recovers the decision benefit of reference evidence. Bio-GRACE shows that retrieval utility is source-dependent, motivates selective retrieval, and exposes why retrieval recall and lexical evidence overlap are insufficient for biomedical fact-checking.

</details>


### [178] [QR-Erase: Efficient Subspace-Based Machine Unlearning with Layer Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01422)
*Tyler Lizzo,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QR-Erase框架，利用Pivoted QR分解替代计算昂贵的SVD来实现机器学习中的"遗忘"任务，通过识别并移除模型参数中与特定任务相关的表征。进一步提出Layer-Localized QR-Erase，仅对包含最高任务特定信息浓度的层进行更新，在多种遗忘场景下取得了优于优化方法的效果，性能与SVD方法差距在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习遗忘方法存在两个主要问题：基于优化的方法会损害模型的其他能力；基于子空间的方法依赖计算昂贵的奇异值分解（SVD）。需要一种既能准确恢复任务特定子空间、又计算高效的替代方案。

Method: 1) 提出QR-Erase框架，使用Pivoted QR分解从模型参数中识别并移除任务特定表征；2) 提出Layer-Localized QR-Erase变体，将更新限制在包含最高任务特定信息浓度的层；3) 理论分析证明Pivoted QR具有有界误差的子空间恢复能力，且在温和谱间隙条件下可接近最优SVD解。

Result: 在任务级遗忘、跨语言遗忘和语音遗忘三种场景下，QR-Erase的遗忘-保留权衡优于优化方法，与SVD在所有指标上的差距在5%以内。利用低秩和层局部化结构进一步提升了遗忘效果（例如语音遗忘集的准确率从53.1%降至15.7%）。

Conclusion: 准确的子空间恢复（而非最优重构）足以实现有效的机器学习遗忘，QR-Erase为现代基础模型提供了一种比SVD方法更高效、更通用的替代方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove targeted information from trained models without requiring costly retraining. Existing optimization-based methods often degrade unrelated capabilities, while subspace-based approaches rely on computationally expensive singular value decompositions (SVD). We introduce QR-Erase, a subspace-based framework that uses Pivoted QR decomposition to identify and remove task-specific representations directly from model parameters. We further propose Layer-Localized QR-Erase, which restricts updates to layers containing the highest concentration of task-specific information. We show that Pivoted QR provides accurate subspace recovery with bounded error, and that under a mild spectral gap condition, the recovered subspace approaches the optimal SVD solution. Across task-level, cross-lingual, and speech unlearning, QR-Erase achieves a stronger forgetting-retention tradeoff than optimization-based methods while remaining within 5% of SVD across all metrics. Exploiting low-rank and layer-localized structure further improves forgetting (for example, reducing speech forget-set accuracy from 53.1% to 15.7%). These results demonstrate that accurate subspace recovery, rather than optimal reconstruction, is sufficient for effective unlearning and provides an efficient and general alternative to SVD-based methods for modern foundation models.

</details>


### [179] [PALMs: Using Multi Construct-Grounded Rationales for Modeling Population Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01458)
*Priyanka Dey,Brihi Joshi,Preyashi Poddar,Jieyu Zhao,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PALMs（Population Aligned Language Models），即面向特定人群对齐的语言模型套件，覆盖美国、印度、巴西、法国和意大利五个国家。通过基于心理学和文化构念合成推理，并将其作为偏好调优过程中的潜在监督信号，实现对不同人群的系统性对齐。实验在人格、价值观与信念、文化规范、道德四个维度上均取得显著提升，平均相对改进8.59%，并在下游任务（个性化奖励建模、人群模拟、社会推理）中展现良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于模拟个体用户行为，但忠实表征一个群体需要捕捉区分不同人群之间在价值观、信念和文化规范上的系统性差异。现有方法未能充分将心理学和文化构念作为归纳偏置引入模型对齐过程中。

Method: 提出PALMs框架，核心包括：（1）面向五个国家的特定人群分别构建对齐模型；（2）合成基于心理学和文化构念的推理（rationales），将其作为偏好调优（preference tuning）中的潜在监督信号，实现人群特定的对齐；（3）在人格、价值观与信念、文化规范、道德四个维度上进行系统评估，并与多种基线（包括文化专门化模型）比较。

Result: PALMs在所有五个国家人群、四个评估维度上一致超越基线，相较最佳基线平均相对提升8.59%。构念驱动的推理优于人口统计提示和基于调查数据的微调。下游应用中，个性化奖励建模提升5.19%，人群模拟提升6.34%，并展现对社会推理任务的强迁移能力。

Conclusion: 将偏好学习扎根于心理学和文化构念，比基于表层响应分布的监督信号提供更丰富的归纳信号。PALMs证明了面向特定人群的系统性对齐可有效提升LLM人群模拟的真实性和下游任务泛化能力，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are being extensively used to simulate individual user behavior, yet faithfully representing a population requires capturing the systematic variation in values, beliefs, and cultural norms that distinguish one group from another. We introduce Population Aligned Language Models (PALMs), a suite of models each aligned to specific populations, covering five countries: USA, India, Brazil, France and Italy. PALMs are created by synthesizing rationales grounded in psychological and cultural constructs and using these as latent supervision during preference tuning for population-specific alignment. Evaluated across four dimensions: personality, values and beliefs, cultural norms, and morality, PALMs consistently outperform baselines, including culture-specialized models, achieving an average of 8.59% relative improvement over the best baseline across all five populations. Notably, construct-grounded rationales outperform both demographic prompting and survey-based fine-tuning, suggesting that grounding preference learning in psychology and culture provides a richer inductive signal than surface-level response distributions. We further demonstrate strong generalization to downstream applications with- out task-specific supervision: outperforming best baselines by 5.19% in personalized reward modeling, 6.34% in population simulation, and showing strong transfer to social reasoning tasks. Datasets and code are available at: https://github.com/limenlp/PALMs.

</details>


### [180] [Retrieval Augmented Biomedical Question Answering with Weak Question Recovery and Neural Reranking for BioASQ Task 14b](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01468)
*Xueying Zhao,Lee Mai,Balaji Anandganesh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DS@GT ARC BioASQ团队构建的医学问答管道系统，通过多源查询扩展、神经重排序、检索优化和OpenBioLLM生成回答，并针对弱检索问题引入条件性恢复策略和检索后剪枝，显著提升了困难问题集上的MAP@10性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学问答中，部分复杂查询的初始检索性能较弱，导致下游答案生成质量受限，需要开发针对弱查询的恢复策略和检索后优化手段来提升整体鲁棒性。

Method: 系统集成了多源查询扩展、PubMed检索、基于MiniLM的语义重排序、RRF融合和特征相关性打分；针对弱查询引入条件性恢复策略（语义扩展、关系感知增强、选择性结果合并），并采用检索后剪枝去除冗余低相关片段，最后通过输出验证和后处理确保格式一致性。

Result: 在BioASQ评估批次上，所提出的恢复和清理策略显著改善了困难问题集的检索鲁棒性和MAP@10性能。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了针对弱查询的恢复策略和检索后剪枝在生物医学问答管道中的有效性，为后续BioASQ评估提供了可靠的系统基础。

Abstract: This work presents DS@GT ARC BioASQ team's work for a biomedical question answering pipeline, integrating multi-source query expansion, neural reranking, retrieval refinement, and OpenBioLLM-assisted answer generation. The system combines PubMed retrieval with fine-tuned MiniLM-based semantic reranking, Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), and feature-based relevance scoring to improve document ranking quality. To address challenging queries with weak retrieval performance, we introduce a conditional weak-question recovery strategy that applies semantic expansion, relationship-aware augmentation, and selective result merging. A post-retrieval pruning stage further removes redundant or low-relevance snippets while preserving evidence coverage for downstream answer generation. Experimental results on BioASQ evaluation batches demonstrate that the proposed recovery and cleanup strategies substantially improve retrieval robustness and MAP@10 performance on difficult question sets. The final system also incorporates output validation and post-processing steps to ensure formatting consistency and submission reliability across BioASQ phases.

</details>


### [181] [Two-Stage Bengali Sentiment Classification: Domain Adaptation Through Continual Learning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01471)
*MD Shaikh Rahman,Syed Maudud E Rabbi,Muhammad Mahbubur Rashid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SentiBanglaBERT，一个两阶段的孟加拉语情感分类框架，结合领域自适应持续预训练和参数高效微调（LoRA），并集成SHAP可解释性分析，在保持计算效率的同时提供了与传统强基线相媲美的稳定性能，并揭示了孟加拉语形态特征对情感预测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（尤其是孟加拉语）的情感分析面临领域特定数据稀缺的挑战，现有方法在准确性与计算效率之间难以平衡，且缺乏对模型预测行为的可解释性理解。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段进行领域自适应的持续预训练以适应新闻风格数据，第二阶段通过低秩自适应（LoRA）实现参数高效微调；同时集成SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）进行模型可解释性分析，揭示孟加拉语形态线索（如否定后缀和体标记）对情感预测的影响。

Result: 实验表明该框架在性能上与强基线方法相当且表现稳定，同时提供了更强的透明度和解释深度。

Conclusion: 领域自适应持续学习可以作为形态丰富、代表性不足的语言中可解释且资源高效NLP的基础，展现了重要的应用潜力。

Abstract: Understanding sentiment in low-resource languages remains a key challenge for Natural Language Processing (NLP), particularly when domain-specific data is scarce. In this work, we present SentiBanglaBERT, a two-stage Bengali sentiment classification framework combining domain-adaptive continual pretraining and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The approach enables contextual adaptation to news-style data while remaining computationally efficient through Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Beyond performance, SentiBanglaBERT integrates SHAP-based interpretability, offering linguistic insights into how Bengali morphological cues, such as negation suffixes and aspectual markers, influence sentiment predictions. Experiments demonstrate stable performance comparable to strong baselines while providing greater transparency and interpretive depth. This framework highlights the potential of domain-adaptive continual learning as a foundation for interpretable, resource-efficient NLP in morphologically rich, underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [182] [Discriminative Axis, Not Data Volume: What a Contrastive Corpus Teaches an Audio Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01560)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，当对比学习音频表示缺失某属性时，扩大语料规模并非有效手段。通过向冻结基座多模态嵌入模型添加词汇-语音轮次，零样本关键词识别提升76点，但语音情感识别下降14点。研究表明，损失并非容量限制，也非数据量问题，而是语料结构决定——对比目标仅在批内负样本无法分离时才会编码某属性。受控语料固定句子内容使韵律成为唯一区分信号，而挖掘语料虽标注情感但场景内容仍可分离。通过干预验证因果性：折叠标题多样性使情感成为唯一分离轴，可恢复8.9点情感识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当对比表示缺失某属性时，扩大语料规模是默认解决方案，但本文报告了一个扩大语料无效的案例，并识别出真正有效的因素——语料结构而非规模或词汇量，揭示对比学习编码属性的真实机制。

Method: 采用冻结基座多模态嵌入模型，添加词汇-语音回合；使用7,442条韵律受控语料进行微调，以及29,428条显式标注情感的挖掘语料进行对比实验；通过控制批内负样本的分离难度、折叠标题多样性等干预手段验证因果性。

Result: 添加词汇-语音轮次使零样本关键词识别提升76点但情感识别下降14点；7,442条韵律受控语料微调可恢复情感并仅损失5点关键词性能；29,428条挖掘语料在匹配曝光下情感仅变化-0.0007；折叠标题多样性使情感恢复8.9点（三粒种子），关键词准确率相应下降。

Conclusion: 对比音频嵌入编码的属性由语料结构决定，而非语料规模或标题词汇量。只有当批内负样本无法通过其他信号分离时，对比目标才会编码目标属性。这一发现为多模态对比学习的语料设计提供了结构性指导。

Abstract: Scaling the corpus is the default remedy when a contrastive representation lacks an attribute. We report a case where it does nothing, and identify what does: adding a lexical-speech round to a frozen-base multimodal embedding model raises zero-shot keyword spotting by 76 points while reducing speech-emotion recognition by 14. The loss is not a capacity limit: fine-tuning on 7,442 clips from a prosody-controlled corpus recovers emotion past its pre-speech level at a five-point keyword cost. Nor is it data volume: 29,428 mined clips whose captions explicitly name emotions, at matched exposure, move emotion by -0.0007. The difference is structural: a contrastive objective encodes an attribute only when the in-batch negatives cannot be separated without it; the controlled corpus holds sentence content fixed, so prosody is the only separating signal, whereas mined captions name emotion yet remain separable by scene content. Intervention on the same audio confirms causality: raising caption similarity does not recover emotion, but collapsing caption diversity so that emotion becomes the only separating axis recovers it by 8.9 points across three seeds, with a smaller, same-signed gain on a non-acted corpus, while keyword accuracy trades back. Corpus structure, not size or caption vocabulary, controls what a contrastive audio embedding encodes.

</details>


### [183] [DocNavRAG: Document-Structured Graph RAG with Stateful Evidence Construction for Complex Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01565)
*Dongyang Xie,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Yifei Yuan,Tieyun Qian,Ming Zhong,Jiawei Jiang,Yuanyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DocNavRAG，一种基于可导航图结构的智能体RAG方法，通过组织文档层级和跨区域关系，提供定位、导航、扩展和获取等图操作，并维护动态证据状态来引导检索，在四个长文档和多文档QA基准上显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG采用固定遍历方式，灵活性不足；而智能体RAG在弱结构接口上反复搜索，效率低下。作者认为智能体应该利用文档内部及跨文档的结构进行导航，而非每次从头搜索。

Method: DocNavRAG将文档层级和跨区域关系组织为可导航图，提供定位（locating）、导航（navigating）、扩展（expanding）和获取（fetching）等图操作接口，并维护一个动态演化的证据状态，持续指导检索过程，直至收集到充分证据。

Result: 在四个长文档和多文档QA基准上，DocNavRAG相比最强基线在答案质量和上下文充分性上分别平均提升7.8%和17.7%。

Conclusion: 通过结构化导航替代反复搜索，DocNavRAG有效提升了复杂文档问答的证据组装能力和检索效率，验证了在文档结构中进行智能体导航的有效性。

Abstract: Answering complex questions over large document collections requires assembling complementary evidence across sections and documents. GraphRAG offers structured retrieval but typically uses fixed traversal, while agentic RAG operates over weakly structured interfaces. Our key insight is that agents should navigate document structure within and across documents rather than repeatedly search from scratch. We introduce DocNavRAG, which organizes document hierarchies and cross-region relations into a navigable graph, exposes graph operations for locating, navigating, expanding, and fetching, and maintains an evolving evidence state to guide retrieval until sufficient evidence is collected. Across four long- and multi-document QA benchmarks, DocNavRAG improves answer quality and context sufficiency over the strongest baseline by 7.8\% and 17.7\% on average.

</details>


### [184] [Characterizing Treatment-Context Medication Evidence Across Clinic Notes and Structured EHR Medication History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01570)
*Mingyang Jiang,Congning Ni,Weixin Liu,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于笔记（note-grounded）的药物信息对比方法，利用大语言模型辅助参考构建、定向与随机人工审查、确定性药物归一化以及语义和时间比较，系统评估了临床笔记与结构化电子健康记录（EHR）药物史之间的信息不一致性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床笔记和结构化EHR药物史常包含不同的药物信息，同一就诊期间两者可能因归一化错误、术语差异、时间差异或文档记录差异而产生分歧，需要系统性方法量化并区分这些分歧来源。

Method: 采用LLM辅助参考构建、定向和随机人工审查、确定性药物归一化，以及语义和时间比较方法；在患者层面的留出测试集（5,403条提及行）上评估所有归一化结果，并进行了全队列特征分析及基于OMOP本体的敏感性分析。

Result: 精确典型匹配从0.7226提升至0.8429；随机审计中典型标签一致性为0.9210，治疗行为归因较低为0.5326；全队列中仅16.44%的笔记行与结构化药物史有精确重叠，55.17%有语义重叠，90.34%有同就诊或±30天重叠，仅3.97%完全无结构化重叠；OMOP支撑的无重叠率从43.99%降至36.68%。

Conclusion: 笔记与结构化药物史之间的不匹配主要源于归一化错误、术语差异和文档记录时间差异三类因素，通过改进归一化和术语映射可以显著提升药物信息的一致性。

Abstract: Clinic notes and structured electronic health record (EHR) medication history often contain different medication information. Same-visit disagreement between these sources may result from note-side normalization errors, differences in terminology or timing, or actual differences in documentation. We developed a note-grounded approach that uses large language model (LLM) assisted reference construction, targeted and random human review, deterministic medication normalization, and semantic and temporal comparisons with structured medication history. We evaluated all normalization results on a patient-level held-out test set to limit adaptation to the study cohort. On 5,403 held-out mention rows, exact canonical agreement improved from 0.7226 with surface-exact matching to 0.8429 after lexical cleanup and curated alias mapping. In a random audit of previously unaudited rows, canonical-label agreement was 0.9210 among evaluable valid medication mentions, whereas treatment-action attribution was lower at 0.5326. In the full-cohort characterization analysis, only 16.44% of note-derived rows had same-visit exact overlap with structured medication history, but 55.17% had same-visit semantic overlap, 90.34% had same-visit or +/-30-day overlap, and only 3.97% remained in the strict no-structured-overlap bucket under broad project-level mapping. An ontology-backed sensitivity analysis further showed that held-out strict Observational Medical Outcomes Partnership (OMOP)-backed no-overlap fell from 43.99% to 36.68% after a development-derived alias supplement. These results show that note-to-structured-medication mismatch can arise from normalization errors, differences in terminology, and differences in documentation timing.

</details>


### [185] [Semantic Alignment of AI Models: Concept Collapse, Checkpoint Dynamics, and Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01585)
*Tyler Ashoff,Jordan Rodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用拓扑方法比较语言模型的高维嵌入空间与低维可解释基线（如本体论和知识图谱），从而实现多模态对齐测试，用于追踪模型适应性和跨语言短语理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型基准测试存在局限：仅测试输出推理无法检验模型对语言的概念化理解，且开源基准容易饱和或被用作训练数据。高维嵌入空间难以直接解释，需要更严格的方法来比较模型如何关联抽象概念。

Method: 利用拓扑方法（topological methods）对语言模型的高维嵌入空间与低维可解释基线（如本体论和策展知识图谱）进行严格的比较，构建多模态对齐测试。

Result: 所提出的多模态对齐测试能够追踪模型的适应变化，并测试模型在多种语言中的短语理解能力。

Conclusion: 拓扑方法为语言模型嵌入空间的评估提供了新的可解释性视角，补充了传统输出层面的基准测试，有助于更深入地理解模型对抽象概念和跨语言语义的建模。

Abstract: Language model benchmarking is a difficult task. Outcome reasoning alone does not test the model's conceptualization of language and popular open-source benchmarks are quickly saturated or ingested as training data. It is important to test the model's output, but augmenting these tests by characterizing semantic structure gives more insight to how models relate abstract concepts. However, the high dimensional embedding spaces are not easy to interpret. This work demonstrates how topological methods can be used to rigorously compare these spaces to low dimensional and interpretable baselines like ontologies and curated knowledge graphs. These multi-modal alignment tests make it possible to track model adaptations and test phrase understanding across multiple languages.

</details>


### [186] [PICTURE: Enhancing Theory-of-Mind in Large Language Models by Revealing, Not Hiding, Characters' Lack of Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01598)
*Eojin Jeon,SangKeun Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PICTURE提示方法，通过让LLM在自由形式的思维链中显式生成角色对事件的无知状态，避免传统事件隐藏方法带来的性能下降问题，在错误信念任务上平均提升7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模拟人类心理理论（ToM）方法依赖事件隐藏（视角取用）推理步骤来移除角色未知的事件，但严格的输出格式约束导致性能下降。而直接采用自由形式的视角取用输出又面临新挑战：LLM需要抑制对角色未知事件的响应。作者假设并验证了角色对事件缺乏了解若在推理过程中被显式化，LLM可以实现这种抑制。

Method: 提出PICTURE提示方法，使LLM在自由形式的思维链（CoT）中显式生成角色对事件缺乏知识的信息，从而在不进行事件隐藏的情况下实现视角取用和正确推理。

Result: PICTURE在错误信念任务上平均比现有提示方法提升7.3%的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式化角色知识缺失状态，可以让LLM在自由格式推理中有效抑制对未知事件的响应，从而规避事件隐藏带来的格式约束性能损失，为NLP中的心理理论建模提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Simulating human-like Theory of Mind (ToM) has been a longstanding problem in natural language processing (NLP). To address this, existing works introduce a reasoning step of event hiding (a.k.a. perspective-taking), where events unknown to a character are removed before question answering. However, resorting to event hiding for ToM reasoning presents a performance degradation issue due to the strict output format constraints involved in event hiding. To mitigate this issue, we propose generating perspective-taking outputs as free-form explanations without event hiding, but this poses a notable yet underexplored challenge: LLMs need to inhibit responses to events unknown to characters, because the absence of event hiding exposes LLMs to these events throughout reasoning. To address this challenge, we hypothesize and empirically verify that LLMs can achieve such inhibition if a character's lack of knowledge about events is made explicit during reasoning. Based on this finding, we introduce PICTURE, a new prompting method that enables LLMs to generate a character's lack of knowledge within free-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Experimental results show that PICTURE outperforms existing prompting methods by an average of 7.3% on false-belief tasks.

</details>


### [187] [Not the Dimension, the Norm: What Matters in Gradient-Free Weight Perturbation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01624)
*Taeyeong Kim,Ahhyun Kim,TaeHyeon Kim,Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过单因素干预实验，系统解耦了梯度自由适配中搜索维度、子空间选择和扰动范数三个因素，发现真正影响性能的核心因素是扰动范数而非子空间方向。12-16个标量扰动比全权重搜索平均低1.8个准确率点，但随机子空间与SVD子空间在匹配尺度因子后表现一致，大尺度下SVD方向先崩溃。范数的可用范围在七种模型间仅关闭五倍，且平坦稳定，设计问题从"扰动哪个子空间"收敛为"如何设置扰动强度"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效方法已将可训练参数从数十亿降至少量标量，但梯度自由适配（随机扰动权重并保留高分者）仍然扰动每个权重张量元素。现有方法的搜索空间、扰动尺度和聚合方式同时变化，导致无法确定全权重搜索是否必要，更无法回答"何种扰动属性使其有效"这一根本问题。

Method: 在固定流水线中逐一干预单个因素，保持候选评分和投票不变，分别变化搜索维度、承载扰动的子空间及其范数。比较12-16个标量冻结帧与全权重搜索的表现，并通过Grassmann重叠度检验随机帧与SVD帧的方向差异，匹配尺度因子后对比性能。

Result: 跨49个模型-基准组合，12-16个标量扰动平均落后全权重搜索1.8个准确率点，在36个组合中落后。维度或基座选择无法解释性能差异：随机帧与SVD帧的Grassmann重叠处于随机水平时，匹配单一尺度因子后表现相同；大尺度下SVD方向先崩溃。唯一存活的因素是扰动范数，其可用范围在七种模型间仅关闭五倍且内部平坦。

Conclusion: 扰动范数是唯一具有失效模式的因素，其安全区域可跨模型规模和族系迁移。设计问题的焦点应从"扰动哪个子空间"收窄为"扰动多大幅度"，为梯度自由适配的参数高效化提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Adapting a language model to a task no longer requires training all of its weights, and a line of parameter-efficient methods has driven the trainable count from billions down to a handful of scalars. Gradient-free adaptation, which samples random weight perturbations and keeps the ones that score well, has not followed that trajectory and still perturbs every entry of the weight tensor. It is unknown whether that full-weight search is necessary, and more fundamentally which property of a perturbation makes it work at all, because existing methods vary the search space, the perturbation scale, and the aggregation together. We resolve this by intervening on one factor at a time inside a fixed pipeline, holding candidate scoring and voting constant while we vary the search dimension, the subspace that carries the perturbation, and its norm. Perturbing a frozen frame of 12 to 16 scalars stays 1.8 accuracy points behind full-weight search on average across 49 model-benchmark cells, trailing it in 36 of them. Neither the dimension nor the choice of basis explains that performance. A random frame whose Grassmann overlap with the SVD frame is at chance level performs identically once a single scale factor is matched, and at large scales the SVD directions collapse first. What survives is the perturbation norm, whose usable range closes within a factor of five across seven models and stays flat inside. The perturbation norm is therefore the one factor with a failure mode, and its safe region transfers across scale and family. The design question narrows from which subspace to perturb to how hard to shake.

</details>


### [188] [Human-LLM Alignment in Language Attitudes Toward Non-Native Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01629)
*Naho Orita,Hayato Ogawa,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类和大型语言模型（LLM）对日语母语者与二语写作者所写邮件的评估，发现LLM在流畅性、地位和团结三个维度上重现了人类评估者的偏误，但呈衰减形式，且模型在团结维度上低估差异，并错误地区分了学习者的母语背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于评估高利害场景中的人类写作（如招聘和学术评估），这使非母语写作者面临被系统性低估的风险。然而，目前对LLM评估偏误的研究主要集中于英语，缺少多语言视角和系统的理论框架来审计这一偏误。

Method: 该研究基于语言态度框架，使用日本人评分员和六个LLM法官，对母语日语（L1）和二语日语（L2）写作者所写的平行邮件文本，在流畅性、地位和团结三个维度上进行评估比较。

Result: 日本评分员在三个维度上对L2文本的评分均显著较低，其中流畅性差距约为地位和团结差距的两倍。六个LLM法官中，五个重现了偏误的方向，五个重现了维度间的排序；但所有模型都低估了团结维度（最具社会根基性的维度）的差距，且所有模型都对学习者母语背景进行了人类没有做出的区分。

Conclusion: LLM法官以结构化但衰减的形式再现了母语者的语言态度，语言态度框架为审计LLM评估偏误提供了一个现成的标尺，且该框架可推广至英语以外的语言场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly evaluate human writing in high-stakes domains such as hiring and academic assessment, putting non-native speakers at particular risk. Drawing on the language attitudes framework, we compared human and LLM evaluations of parallel L1- and L2-written Japanese emails on three dimensions: fluency, status, and solidarity. Japanese raters rated L2 texts significantly lower on all three dimensions, with a fluency gap roughly twice the size of the status and solidarity gaps. Six LLM judges reproduced the direction of this bias, and five reproduced its ordering across dimensions. The models diverged from humans in two ways: all understated the solidarity gap, the most socially grounded dimension, and all differentiated among learner L1 backgrounds where humans did not. LLM judges thus reproduce native speakers' language attitudes in a structured yet attenuated form, and the language attitudes framework offers a ready-made yardstick for auditing them beyond English.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Axient: Debt-Free Finality for Leveraged Binary Event Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00631)
*Maksym Nechepurenko*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Axient，一个用于二元事件市场的实体抵押保证金层协议，将杠杆到期与索赔到期分离，并在明确的执行不确定性下处理硬平仓决策。协议在决策时选择最小卖出量，其底层结算收益包络覆盖债务上限加缓冲。论文证明了稳健的先验债务清算、路径债务消除、无债务终局不变量等性质，并给出了确定性验证器覆盖多种交易场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杠杆事件头寸将可偿还贷款与可能在进行预言机赔付前变得不可交易的结果索赔相结合，这带来了独特的风险和清算挑战。现有方法未能在执行不确定性和市场关闭的情况下给出原理性的硬平仓决策机制。

Method: 协议在决策时选择满足"最小卖出量"条件的最优操作，其下界结算收益包络覆盖了结算时点的债务上界加缓冲。模型区分了报价账面收益、匹配收益、结算收益和赎回，并定义了四个稳健不变量。同时推导了订单簿依赖的杠杆包络、无重复计数的聚合硬平仓容量以及场景条件储备界限。提供了一个确定性验证器以覆盖多种边界情形。

Result: 证明了稳健的先验债务清算、路径债务消除和债务自由终局不变量，最大残余现货敞口，赔付向量与争议时长不变性，以及在执行、签名者控制、结算或市场关闭使注册操作集失效时的不可能性边界。确定性验证器覆盖了分步订单簿、部分成交、结算延迟、对抗性簿变换、共享簿清算、储备分配、零流动性和多种赔付向量等场景。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个条件性机制设计结果和参考实现边界，而非生产安全的声明。运行与压力集由作者指定，实证校准作为独立工作。

Abstract: Leveraged event positions combine a repayable loan with an outcome claim that may become non-tradable before oracle payout is final. This paper specifies Axient, a physically backed margin layer for binary event markets that separates leverage maturity from claim maturity and makes the hard-flat decision under explicit execution uncertainty. The model distinguishes quoted book proceeds, matched proceeds, settled proceeds, and redemption. At decision time, the protocol selects the smallest sale whose lower settled-proceeds envelope covers an upper bound on debt at the settlement horizon plus a buffer. We prove robust ex-ante debt clearing, pathwise debt-extinguishment and debt-free-finality invariants, maximal residual spot exposure, payout-vector and dispute-duration invariance of lender principal after debt extinction, and an impossibility boundary when execution, signer control, settlement, or market closure leave the registered operating set. We also derive a book-dependent leverage envelope, aggregate hard-flat capacity without double-counting shared liquidity, and scenario-conditional reserve bounds. A deterministic verifier covers step books, partial fills, settlement delay, adversarial book transformations, shared-book liquidation, reserve allocation, zero liquidity, and multiple payout vectors. The operating and stress sets are author-specified; empirical calibration is separate. The contribution is a conditional mechanism-design result and reference-implementation boundary, not a production-safety claim.

</details>


### [190] [Axient: On-Chain Credit and Loss Allocation for Leveraged Event Markets: A Venue-Agnostic Protocol for Traders, Credit Providers, Market Makers, and Liquidation Backstops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00647)
*Maksym Nechepurenko*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向链上杠杆事件头寸的、与具体交易场所无关的信用架构，并构建了其内生资本市场的数学模型，通过大规模确定性模拟与Agent-Based实验验证了机制的稳健性与风险传播特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理支撑的杠杆事件头寸需要真实的信用支持：当抵押品C获得杠杆L时，协议需提供(L-1)C的信贷资金。现有链上架构缺乏将这一资本层与具体交易场所解耦的系统化设计与内生市场模型，且对参与者行为（提款、清算、挤兑、传染）缺乏形式化刻画。

Method: 论文设计了一个与场所无关的链上信用架构，区分交易者、高级信用LP、做市商、清算人和清算后援提供者五类角色；形式化定义池份额与债务份额、利用率和风险敏感的利率、抵押锁定职位账户、场所能力、做市商承诺、提款队列、隔离池、不可赎回储备与确定性损失瀑布；并建模内生参与者参与、杠杆需求、流动性提款、清算人进入、储备补充、挤兑与共同因子传染。同时通过固定种子Agent-Based实验进行大规模标量不变量验证。

Result: 形式化结果表明：实数和整数单位记账平衡、结算确认的债务优先权、交易者剩余所有权、幂等部分结算、非稀释份额发行、次级先于高级损失、损失参与的提款队列、利用率均衡条件以及损失分配与传染边界均得到保证。实验保留28个精确fixture和31,082个确定性检查，新增70,207,488次标量不变量评估零失败。实验显示分层保护降低但未消除高级损失，5倍杠杆显著增加资本压力，做市商容量若过快扩张可能推高总缺口。

Conclusion: 该机制在数学一致性与极端场景稳健性上表现良好，但结果仅是作者设定行为下的合成机制比较，不构成对APY、违约、场所流动性的预测或生产安全性的保证。

Abstract: A physically backed leveraged event position requires real credit: if collateral C receives leverage L, the protocol supplies (L-1)C and uses the combined amount to acquire recognized event exposure. This paper develops a venue-agnostic on-chain credit architecture for that capital layer and an endogenous model of its capital market. It separates traders, Senior Credit LPs, market makers, liquidators, and Liquidation Backstop Providers; formalizes pool and debt shares, utilization- and risk-sensitive interest, collateral-locked position accounts, venue capabilities, market-maker commitments, withdrawal queues, isolated pools, non-redeemable reserves, and a deterministic loss waterfall; and models endogenous provider participation, leverage demand, liquidity withdrawal, liquidator entry, reserve replenishment, runs, and common-factor contagion. Formal results establish balanced real- and integer-unit accounting, settlement-confirmed debt priority, trader residual ownership, idempotent partial settlement, non-dilutive share issuance, junior-before-Senior impairment, loss-participating withdrawal queues, utilization-equilibrium conditions, and loss-allocation and contagion bounds. The release preserves 28 exact fixtures and 31,082 deterministic checks and adds fixed-seed agent-based experiments with 70,207,488 scalar invariant evaluations and zero failures. The experiments show that layered protection reduces but does not eliminate Senior loss, that 5x leverage materially increases capital pressure, and that market-maker capacity can raise aggregate shortfall if admission expands too quickly. Results are synthetic mechanism comparisons under author-specified behavior, not forecasts of APY, defaults, venue liquidity, or production safety.

</details>


### [191] [Optimal Trading of Microstructure Mean Reversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00885)
*Lucas Rabechini Amaral*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文研究在秒级时间尺度上，中间价围绕潜在有效价格呈现平稳的均值回归误差。作者构建了一个订单簿模型来产生该误差，并求解最大化长期平均利润率的交易规则。在流动性好的大tick资产中，价差为1或2个tick，其奇偶性与中间价在半tick网格上的位置相关。通过一个平衡响应条件的假设，证明间隙G的均值回归性质与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程完全相同，并给出了最优交易带半宽θ*和利润率R*的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在秒级时间尺度上，市场中间价与潜在有效价格之间存在一个可观测的、均值回归的误差。作者希望理解并利用这一结构，构建一个能产生该误差的订单簿模型，并求解在该框架下的最优交易策略。

Method: 作者构建一个订单簿模型，其中中间价为纯跳跃过程，其跳跃强度偏向有效价格。在"平衡响应条件"假设下，证明间隙G的条件均值和稳态协方差与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程精确匹配。然后将该高斯扩散作为替代过程来评估通行时间，并求解对称交易带的最优半宽。最优半宽满足θ*(θ*-φ)=s_G²，最优利润率为R*=αs_G√(2/π)e^{-θ*²/2s_G²}。

Result: 在替代高斯过程上，对称带策略在所有可容许策略中是最优的。最优半宽θ*由θ*(θ*-φ)=s_G²给出，对应的最大利润率为R*=αs_G√(2/π)e^{-θ*²/2s_G²}。阈值时间与间隙的平稳方差相等。当间隙刚覆盖价差时交易收益为零，所有利润都来自等待的期权价值。

Conclusion: 间隙G的均值回归特征可以被精确刻画（与OU过程等价），但路径行为不同（中间价跳跃）。对称带策略在替代扩散上最优，但在原始跳跃过程上仍是一个猜想。交易的利润完全来源于等待的期权价值，而非即时覆盖价差。

Abstract: At the scale of seconds the observed mid carries a stationary, mean-reverting error around a latent efficient price. We build an order book whose own flow produces that error and solve for the trading rule that maximises the long-run average profit rate net of the bid-ask spread. In a liquid large-tick asset the spread is one tick or two, and it is exactly the parity of the mid on the half-tick grid: tight at a half-integer, open at an integer. One coordinate therefore carries the problem: the gap $G$ between the mid and the efficient price; the price is an exogenous Brownian martingale, and $G$ is observable. The mid is a pure jump process whose move intensities lean toward the efficient price. Under one balanced-response condition, which equalises the book's corrective drift across parities, mean reversion of $G$ is a theorem: its conditional mean and stationary covariance are exactly those of an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process with reversion rate $α$ and stationary standard deviation $s_G$. Its paths are not: the mid jumps. Passage times are therefore evaluated on the Gaussian diffusion those two moments define, at an error we bound on the reward side and leave heuristic on the timing side. A symmetric band of half-width $θ$ buys when the gap reaches $-θ$, sells at $+θ$, and holds inside; on the surrogate it is optimal among all admissible strategies, on the jump process itself that reduction remains a conjecture. With $φ$ the tight-book half-spread, the optimal half-width and its profit rate are $θ^*(θ^*-φ)=s_G^2$ and $R^*=αs_G\sqrt{2/π}\,e^{-θ^{*2}/2s_G^2}$. Threshold times margin equals the stationary variance of the gap. Trading as soon as the gap covers the spread earns zero: all profit is the option value of waiting.

</details>


### [192] [Exactly solvable model for the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under long-range correlated market-order flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00988)
*Yuki Sato,Shunta Fujiwara,Kiyoshi Kanazawa*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文通过将平方根价格冲击定律引入Lillo-Mike-Farmer（LMF）模型，构建了一个可精确求解的非线性时间序列模型，解决了可预测市场订单流与布朗运动价格动态之间的矛盾（扩散价格动态悖论）。该模型可数学映射到Lévy行走框架，并证明在平方根定律下，即使存在可预测的市场订单流，长期价格动态依然是扩散的，强调了平方根定律在有效市场假说的微观结构基础中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场微观结构中存在一个悖论：一方面，长期来看价格动态近似为布朗运动（扩散过程）；另一方面，市场订单流因长程相关性而具有显著的可预测性（由LMF模型数学描述）。由于市场订单通常具有正价格冲击效应，可预测的订单流似乎与布朗价格动态相矛盾。作者旨在解决这一扩散价格动态悖论。

Method: 将平方根价格冲击定律融入LMF模型，构建可精确求解的非线性时间序列模型；这些模型可数学映射到统计物理中Lévy行走框架（一类可精确求解的非马尔可夫随机过程），从而利用该框架进行严格分析和求解。

Result: 证明了在平方根价格冲击定律下，即使市场订单流具有可预测性（长程相关性），长期价格动态仍然是扩散的（即呈现布朗运动行为），从而解决了扩散价格动态悖论。

Conclusion: 平方根价格冲击定律在理解有效市场假说的微观结构基础中具有重要的实践意义，为解释可预测订单流与有效市场中随机价格行为共存提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We develop an exactly solvable nonlinear time-series model by incorporating the square-root price-impact law into the Lillo--Mike--Farmer (LMF) model to resolve the diffusive price-dynamics paradox under predictable market-order flow. In financial market microstructure, it is well established that the price dynamics are approximately described by Brownian motion at long times. However, it is also well-known that market-order flow is clearly predictable due to long-range correlations, as mathematically formulated by the LMF model. Since market orders have a positive price impact in general, predictable market-order flow seems to contradict Brownian price dynamics. In this work, we resolve this diffusive price-dynamics paradox by developing nonlinear time-series models that generalize the LMF model based on the square-root price-impact law. Our time-series models can be mathematically mapped onto the Lévy-walk framework---an exactly solvable class of non-Markovian stochastic processes developed in statistical physics. We prove that the price dynamics are diffusive at long times under the square-root law even under predictable market-order flow. Our work highlights the crucial practical importance of the square-root law in understanding the microstructural foundation of the Efficient Market Hypothesis.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.PM'></div>

# q-fin.PM [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Conformal Kelly: Conformal Prediction Intervals as the Scale in Fractional Kelly Position Sizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01494)
*Robert Jacob Ryan*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出将75%保形预测区间与分数Kelly准则结合用于投资组合仓位管理，在2016-2021开发窗口上实现28.5%的年化净对数增长和1.34的Sharpe比率，但预注册的样本外评估显示增长未能保持，两种配置年化收益仅为8.5%和7.0%，低于被动基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统保形预测仅用于量化预测不确定性，本文希望利用该不确定性信息来动态调整投资组合仓位，从而在预测区间变宽时缩小仓位、收窄时扩大仓位，实现不确定性驱动的自适应投资策略。

Method: 将75%保形预测区间与分数Kelly准则结合，根据区间宽度动态调整仓位大小。采用慢速、未加权、逐资产滚动分位数作为区间计算方法。引入风险控制机制：当下行方向区间失配频率超过历史水平时削减杠杆。使用自主LLM智能体在200个配置中搜索最优方案，并对2022年后的数据进行预注册样本外评估。

Result: 开发窗口（2016-2021）上实现28.5%年化净对数增长、Sharpe比率1.34、最大回撤27.7%，优于S&P 500的15.9%和同杠杆被动组合的21-22%。风险控制将最大回撤从27.7%降至20.3%并提高Sharpe比率。但预注册的样本外评估中，两种配置年化收益仅8.5%和7.0%，低于被动基准，回望检验虽增长更高但伴随46%回撤。

Conclusion: 开发窗口内保形预测区间用于仓位管理表现优异，且宽度稳定性优于局部尖度，简单的慢速滚动分位数超越复杂自适应方法。然而样本外结果揭示了过拟合风险——尽管通过预注册和校准保持（0.745 vs 0.750），增长表现未能持续，凸显了量化策略中样本外验证的重要性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction has traditionally been used to quantify prediction uncertainty. We put that uncertainty to a second use, combining a 75% conformal interval with fractional Kelly to size portfolio positions: as the range widens we shrink the position, and as it narrows we grow it. On a six-year development window (2016-2021), with trading costs and strict leverage caps, this compounds at 28.5% annualised net log growth with a Sharpe ratio of 1.34 and a 27.7% maximum drawdown, versus 15.9% for holding the S&P 500 and 21-22% for passive portfolios at the same leverage.
  Our main development-window finding runs against the literature's advice for conformal prediction on time series. Every tweak that adapts the interval faster to market conditions costs 0.7 to 5.3 points of annual growth; the winner is the simplest method: slow, unweighted, per-asset rolling quantiles. When an interval sizes a position rather than describing one forecast, width stability beats local sharpness. It also beats the textbook standard deviation by 2.1 points at matched leverage. We also implement a risk control: when the intervals miss on the downside far more than their historical rate, we cut leverage. On the development window this cut maximum drawdown from 27.7% to 20.3% while raising the Sharpe ratio, beating all 40 placebo timings (rank-based p = 1/41).
  These numbers came from an autonomous LLM-agent search over 200 configurations, so we sealed all data from 2022 onward and pre-registered configurations, benchmarks, and interpretation rules before one evaluation. Calibration held (0.745 coverage against 0.750, weakest through 2022); growth did not: the two configurations earned 8.5% and 7.0% per year, below the passive benchmarks, and a pre-registered hindsight benchmark beat them on raw growth while taking a 46% drawdown. All outcomes are reported as pre-registered.

</details>


### [194] [Path Portfolio Optimization: Defect, Lift, and the Price of Path Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02355)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出了"路径组合优化"（Path Portfolio Optimization）框架，将组合理论建立于路径优先的框架之上，使用签名（signature）作为价格路径的通用坐标，并探讨该框架在估计情境下是否依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合理论以价格水平为基础，忽略了路径信息。本文试图回答：若将组合视为路径签名的线性泛函，这一推广是否能在实际估计中存活？即理论上的优雅结构是否具有实证可行性。

Method: 将组合定义为签名坐标的线性泛函，控制变量位于截断张量代数中；均值-方差问题被转化为单个张量上的线性系统。利用Marcus与forward lift之间的差距（即Fernholz超额增长率）刻画执行约定效应，并利用level 2反对称块保证路径不变性。

Result: 实证发现存在维度权衡：预期签名已知时，二次路径泛函可分别使一对资产和二十个资产的确定性等价提高11倍和60倍；但在估计情形下，非正则化策略在样本量低于每参数约6个观测值时严重为负。收益几乎全部来自对称块（终端增量的凸性），而非路径依赖的反对称块。基于生成器的估计器在每参数仅约1个观测值时即可恢复几乎全部价值。

Conclusion: 路径签名框架在组合优化中是可行的，但面临样本量约束；该约束源于非结构化估计而非路径复杂度本身。结构化估计（拟合驱动器的生成器）可大幅降低样本需求，且超额增长的来源是终端增量的凸性而非路径依赖。

Abstract: This paper builds Path Portfolio Optimization: portfolio theory on a path-first framework in which the signature is the universal coordinate of the price path, and asks whether it survives estimation. A portfolio is a linear functional of the signature, so the control lives in a truncated tensor algebra, the covariance of signature coordinates is the non-group-like part of the expected signature --- a defect form --- and the whole mean--variance problem becomes a linear system in one tensor. Two structural results follow. The lift is the execution convention: the gap between the Marcus and forward lifts, contracted with portfolio weights, is Fernholz's excess growth rate exactly, so excess growth is the geometricity defect of the portfolio map. And the antisymmetric block at level two is lift-invariant pathwise, so directional signals are convention-free while variance signals and ruin are not. The empirical finding is a dimensional trade-off. With the expected signature known, quadratic path functionals raise the certainty equivalent elevenfold for a pair of assets and sixtyfold for a cross section of twenty; with it estimated, the unregularized policy is severely negative until the sample exceeds roughly six observations per parameter, and shrinkage flips from harmful in the pair to indispensable in the cross section. The entire gain sits in the symmetric block, which is convexity in the terminal increment rather than path-dependence; the path-dependent antisymmetric block earns nothing when the driver has no expected area. And the sample-size floor belongs to unstructured estimation rather than to path complexity: an estimator that fits only the generator of the driver and rebuilds the expected signature recovers almost all of the attainable value at barely one observation per parameter

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Fast Trainable Multilinear Bases for Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00053)
*Shiwen An,Zhongyi Ni,Huanhai Zhou,Jin-Guo Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将离散傅里叶变换和离散余弦变换推广到等距多线性基的方法，通过引入少量参数（随图像尺寸呈多项式对数增长），保持近线性时间、精确可逆和无参这几个关键性质，并利用量子多体理论中的等距张量网络和黎曼优化在流形上训练，以找到对特定数据集压缩效果最佳的基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像和视频编解码器广泛依赖DFT、DCT及其分块变体，这些变换之所以有效是因为它们具有近线性运行时间、精确可逆性和极少参数。然而这些固定基无法根据具体数据集自适应优化，作者希望在不牺牲这三项关键性质的前提下，引入少量可学习参数来提升压缩效率。

Method: 将传统正交变换推广为等距多线性基，采用受量子多体理论启发的等距张量网络进行参数化，并在酉矩阵流形上使用黎曼优化方法训练。参数数量随图像尺寸仅呈polylog增长，从而保留了近线性时间和可逆性等性质。

Result: 在自然照片和线条画数据集上，训练得到的基均优于固定非参数基线。在Quick Draw线条画压缩任务上，与JPEG的8×8分块余弦变换相比，在相同重建质量下存储字节数减少约20%。

Conclusion: 通过该方法，可以在保持传统变换关键优势的同时，实现数据自适应的压缩基学习，显著提升特定类型图像的压缩效率。

Abstract: The Discrete Fourier Transform, the Discrete Cosine Transform, and their block-wise variants underpin most deployed image and video codecs. Their effectiveness rests on three properties: they run in near-linear time (linear up to a polylogarithmic factor), they are exactly invertible, and they carry few to no parameters. In this work, we generalize these bases to isometric multilinear bases, allowing a small number of extra parameters, polylogarithmic in the image size, while preserving all three properties. Given an image dataset, we develop a systematic framework that searches this family for the basis compressing the dataset most effectively: the basis is parameterized as an isometric tensor network, inspired by quantum many-body theory, and trained with Riemannian optimization on the manifold of unitary matrices. Across natural photographs and line drawings, the trained bases consistently improve on their fixed, non-parametric counterparts. On Quick Draw line-drawing compression, they store images in roughly $20\%$ fewer bytes than JPEG's $8 \times 8$ block cosine transform at the same reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [196] [FDIR: Harmonizing Fidelity and Human-Machine Preference in Lossy Compression Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00111)
*Kuan-Yen Chen,Fang-Yi Su,Philip Chikontwe,Jung-Hsien Chiang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出FDIR，一种两阶段图像恢复架构，通过解耦像素保真度、人类感知和机器偏好三个目标，实现三者的平衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有损压缩恢复方法最多只能优化三个质量标准（像素级保真度、人类感知、下游机器偏好）中的一个：保真度导向模型产生过度平滑输出，生成式方法则会产生看似合理但事实错误的纹理，损害保真度和下游任务精度。

Method: FDIR采用两阶段架构，通过互补的归纳偏置解耦冲突需求：1）质量引导的单步流匹配（QO-Flow）在潜在空间中通过单次前向传播恢复全局语义结构；2）流条件细节精炼（FCDR）在像素空间中确定性恢复高频纹理并抑制生成幻觉。

Result: 大量实验表明FDIR在保真度上表现优越，在感知-保真度平衡方面表现良好，同时具有竞争力的机器偏好性能。

Conclusion: FDIR通过两阶段解耦设计有效平衡了图像恢复中的三维权衡（保真度、感知、机器偏好），克服了单一目标优化方法的局限性。

Abstract: Image restoration quality can be evaluated along three complementary facets: pixel-level fidelity, human perception, and downstream machine preference. However, existing lossy compression restoration methods optimize for at most one of these criteria: fidelity-oriented models often regress toward conditional means and produce over-smoothed outputs, while generative approaches hallucinate plausible but factually incorrect textures that degrade both ground-truth fidelity and downstream task accuracy. To navigate this three-way tradeoff, we propose FDIR, a two-stage architecture that decouples the conflicting demands through complementary inductive biases: Quality-Guided One-Step Flow Matching (QO-Flow) recovers global semantic structure in latent space via a single forward pass, while Flow-Conditioned Detail Refinement (FCDR) deterministically restores high-frequency textures and suppresses generative hallucinations in pixel space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FDIR achieves superior fidelity, with a favorable perceptual-fidelity balance and competitive machine preference.

</details>


### [197] [RPL-UIE: Reliable Prior Learning for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00137)
*Yifan Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ye Zheng,Zhe Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出RPL-UIE，一种两阶段师生框架用于水下图像增强，通过可靠先验学习解决生成方法中因信息丢失导致的语义漂移问题，在多个基准测试和下游任务上取得了竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式方法虽能处理复杂的水下退化，但严重的信息丢失会导致恢复结果出现语义漂移，影响增强质量和下游视觉任务性能。

Method: 采用两阶段师生框架：教师阶段从配对图像学习可靠且互补的空间先验（表征外观和光度属性）；学生阶段仅以退化图像为输入，模拟教师的先验提取能力。同时提出残差先验细化扩散（RPRD）利用粗先验作为锚点在残差空间中逐步预测修正，以及频率感知先验残差校准（FPRC）保留稳定低频分量并选择性调制高频细节残差，以缩小师生模型之间的先验学习差距。

Result: 在多个水下图像增强基准上取得竞争性恢复性能；下游水下目标检测和实例分割实验证明了增强图像对视觉感知的改善；在ROV采集的真实数据上的测试验证了实际应用可行性。

Conclusion: RPL-UIE通过可靠的先验学习有效解决了语义漂移问题，不仅提升了水下图像增强质量，还增强了下游任务的实用性和在真实场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement (UIE) aims to recover clear images from observations affected by wavelength-dependent absorption, scattering, and spatially nonuniform degradation. Although existing generative methods can handle complex degradations, severe information loss may lead to semantic drift in the restored results. To address this issue, we propose RPL-UIE, a two-stage teacher--student framework for reliable prior learning. In the teacher stage, the network learns reliable and complementary spatial priors characterizing appearance and photometric properties from paired degraded and reference images. In the student stage, the network takes only degraded images as input and learns to emulate the teacher's prior extraction capability, thereby providing more reliable restoration guidance for the enhancement process without requiring reference images at inference. To reduce the prior-learning discrepancy between the teacher and student models, we further develop Residual Prior Refinement Diffusion (RPRD) and Frequency-Aware Prior Residual Calibration (FPRC). RPRD uses the coarse priors as anchors and progressively predicts the necessary corrections in the residual space. FPRC retains stable low-frequency residual components and selectively modulates high-frequency detail residuals, producing calibrated priors to support high-quality reconstruction. Experiments on multiple UIE benchmarks demonstrate competitive restoration performance. Downstream underwater object detection and instance segmentation experiments further demonstrate the improved utility of enhanced images for visual perception, while tests on real-world data captured by a remotely operated vehicle (ROV) support the practical applicability of RPL-UIE.

</details>


### [198] [Automatic LV Localization and Short-Axis Plane Estimation from Arbitrary CMR Slice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00145)
*Yi Yu,Yixuan Liu,Ziyu Zhang,Parker Martin,Zhenyu Bu,Yuchi Han,Yuan Xue*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的范式：从单个心脏磁共振（CMR）切片中联合进行左心室（LV）定位和3D方向估计，通过Polar-Coupled Circular（PCC）嵌入提供连续且无歧义的方向表示，在四个数据集上达到86.18%的平均mIoU和3.39°的平均角度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LV方向识别方法要么将其视为离散视图分类，要么依赖多切片几何相交，无法建模连续的3D方向，也难以泛化到任意切片。因此需要一种能够从单一切片中同时估计LV位置和连续3D方向的新方法。

Method: 该方法首先将代表性的方向感知检测框架适配到CMR领域并分析其局限性，然后提出Polar-Coupled Circular（PCC）嵌入作为连续且无歧义的方向表示，并通过从体积CMR分割数据集中自动切片采样构建可扩展的基准数据集。

Result: 在四个数据集上的大量实验表明该方法性能优秀，平均mIoU达到86.18%，平均角度偏差仅为3.39°。

Conclusion: 本研究为单切片LV方向建模建立了新的任务设定，并提供了一种几何一致性的框架用于空间信息感知的CMR分析，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of left ventricular (LV) orientation is essential for cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging and downstream analysis. Existing methods typically formulate orientation recognition as discrete view classification or rely on multi-slice geometric intersection, limiting their ability to model continuous 3D orientation and generalize across arbitrary slices. This work introduces a novel paradigm: Joint LV localization and 3D orientation estimation from a single CMR slice. To investigate this setting, representative orientation-aware detection frameworks are adapted to the CMR domain, and their limitations are analyzed. Upon that, we propose the Polar-Coupled Circular (PCC) embedding that provides a continuous and unambiguous orientation representation to address the limitations. Meanwhile, a scalable benchmark is constructed through automatic slice sampling from volumetric CMR segmentation datasets. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate strong performance, achieving an average mIoU of 86.18% and an average angle deviation of 3.39°. This study establishes a new task setting for single-slice LV orientation modeling and provides a geometry-consistent framework for spatially informed CMR analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/yuyi1005/cmr-3d-ood.

</details>


### [199] [MedSAM2-Anatomy: Training-Free Inference-Time Optimization for Musculoskeletal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00195)
*John Garcia Henao,Nicholas Bünger,Benedikt Herzog,Cindy Guerrero Toro,Benjamin Vella,Matthias Biner,Rico Brütsch,Carmen Castroviejo Fernandez,Felix Öttl,Norman Juchler,Armando Hoch,Bettina Hochreiter,Sven Hirsch,Sebastiano Caprara*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedSAM2-Anatomy，一种无需训练的推理时优化框架，通过将冻结的专家分割模型生成的解剖先验自动转化为多种提示假设，输入冻结的3D基础分割模型，融合候选掩膜并拒绝解剖学上不合理的先验，实现无需微调或人工提示的高分辨率髋关节和肩关节3D分割。在独立数据集上，髋部MRI的Dice从0.71提升至0.92，肩部CT从0.89提升至0.92。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D髋关节和肩关节分割对手术规划至关重要，但冻结的分割模型在域偏移下表现不佳。CNN专家模型全自动但缺乏适应性，而可提示的基础模型泛化能力强但需要人工提示，两者均存在明显局限。

Method: 冻结的专家模型（TotalSegmentator）生成解剖先验，自动转换为多个提示假设，输入冻结的3D基础模型（MedSAM2）；候选掩膜进行融合，并拒绝解剖学上不合理的先验。整个过程不更新任何模型权重，也不需要人工提示。

Result: 在独立Balgrist-V0 CT和MRI数据集上，推理时优化将髋部MRI的中位Dice从0.71提升至0.92，肩部CT从0.89提升至0.92，髋部MRI的中位HD95从22.0mm降至5.0mm。在公开TotalSegmentator基准上，专家模型仍然最强，表明最优融合策略取决于专家先验的可靠性。

Conclusion: 训练免费的推理时优化提供了一种实用策略，无需人工提示即可改进冻结的分割模型，在域偏移场景下显著提升分割精度和边界质量。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D segmentation of hip and shoulder anatomy from CT and MRI is essential for surgical planning, yet frozen segmentation models often fail under domain shift. CNN-based expert models are fully automatic but lack adaptability, whereas promptable foundation models generalize better but require manual prompting. We present MedSAM2-Anatomy, a training-free inference-time optimization framework that improves frozen segmentation models without retraining or human interaction. A frozen expert model generates anatomical priors that are automatically converted into multiple prompt hypotheses for a frozen 3D foundation model. Candidate masks are fused while anatomically implausible priors are rejected. No model weights are updated and no manual prompts are required. TotalSegmentator and MedSAM2 are used as representative expert and foundation models, allowing the contribution of the inference policy to be isolated. Evaluation on the independent Balgrist-V0 CT and MRI cohorts shows that inference-time optimization increases median Dice from 0.71 to 0.92 on hip MRI and from 0.89 to 0.92 on shoulder CT, while reducing median HD95 on hip MRI from 22.0 mm to 5.0 mm. On public TotalSegmentator benchmarks, the expert model remains strongest, indicating that the optimal fusion strategy depends on the reliability of the expert prior. These results demonstrate that training-free inference-time optimization provides a practical strategy for improving frozen segmentation models without manual prompting.

</details>


### [200] [Learning to See Locally and Align Clinically with Pathology Semantics for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00279)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出PALM，一种病理感知对齐框架，通过共享病理原型来对齐视觉和文本特征，并引入Masked Evidence Modeling增强图像编码器对局部影像证据的敏感性，在MIMIC-CXR、IU X-Ray和MIMIC-ABN数据集上一致提升了报告生成和异常聚焦的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学视觉-语言模型面临图像与文本对齐不充分的问题：自回归监督难以建立可靠对齐，而对比学习会将描述相关病理但未配对的报告错误推开，导致模型依赖预训练语言先验生成缺乏影像证据支持的临床报告。

Method: 提出PALM框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) 通过共享病理原型对齐视觉和文本特征，使具有相似病理语义的病例向共同概念靠近而非相互排斥；(2) 引入Masked Evidence Modeling，通过掩蔽图像区域来学习语义变化，增强图像编码器对局部放射学证据的敏感度。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR、IU X-Ray和MIMIC-ABN三个数据集上的实验表明，PALM在报告生成任务和异常聚焦的鲁棒性方面均取得了一致性改进。

Conclusion: 通过病理原型对齐替代传统对比学习，能够更有效地组织放射学图像与报告之间的语义关系，从而减轻模型对语言先验的过度依赖，提升报告的临床可靠性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent radiology-adapted vision-language models have achieved strong performance on standard report generation benchmarks, yet their robustness and generalization remain constrained by imperfect alignment and correlation between visual and textual features. Existing methods connect image and text either implicitly through autoregressive report supervision or explicitly through contrastive learning. However, autoregressive supervision alone is insufficient to establish reliable image-text alignment, while contrastive learning can push apart unpaired reports that describe related pathologies simply because they are not paired with the same image. This is problematic in radiology, where different reports may share compatible pathology semantics rather than being true negatives. As a result, the learned representation may fail to organize images and reports around shared pathology concepts, causing the decoder to rely on pretrained language priors and generate clinically plausible reports that are not fully supported by radiographic evidence. To address this issue, we propose PALM, a pathology-aware alignment framework for radiology report generation. Instead of directly matching each image-report pair while separating all others, PALM aligns visual and textual features through shared pathology prototypes. These prototypes provide a clinically meaningful bridge between radiographic evidence and textual findings, allowing cases with similar pathology semantics to move toward common concepts without separating compatible cases. In addition, we introduce Masked Evidence Modeling to strengthen the image encoder sensitivity to local radiographic evidence by learning semantic changes caused by masked image regions. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR, IU X-Ray, and MIMIC-ABN show that PALM consistently improves both report generation and abnormality-focused robustness.

</details>


### [201] [Fused electrical impedance tomography using an electromagnetically tracked handheld probe for oral cancer margin assessment: reconstruction methods and phantom validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00312)
*Sophie A Lloyd,Ehsan Nasiri,Allaire F Doussan,Ryan J Halter,Ethan K Murphy*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种融合电阻抗断层成像（EIT）方法，通过手持式电阻抗探头在多个位置采集阻抗数据，实现口腔鳞状细胞癌（OSCC）肿瘤边界的精确重建，定位精度可达0.5毫米以内，为术中肿瘤边界扫描的临床应用迈出重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔鳞状细胞癌切除手术中阳性切缘（肿瘤残留）很常见，严重影响患者预后。需要一种术中手段来精确区分肿瘤与健康组织并重建肿瘤边界，以改善手术效果。

Method: 通过仿真和噪声分析比较多种融合EIT重建方法，确定最佳方案；使用手持式电阻抗探头在多个位置记录阻抗数据，并通过体模（phantom）实验验证融合EIT重建的边界定位性能。

Result: 融合EIT方法能够从多个探头位置准确恢复电导率边界位置，仿真和实验结果中边界定位误差均缩小至真实边界0.5毫米以内。融合差分EIT（fused difference EIT）对所有类型噪声最稳健，得益于重建公式中的高数据冗余度。

Conclusion: 多位置探头实验验证了融合EIT结合多位置数据提高边界定位精度的可行性，这是向临床可部署的阻抗成像方法扫描整个肿瘤边界迈出的重要一步，有望显著改善OSCC手术结局。

Abstract: Objective: Positive margins in oral squamous cell carcinoma (OSCC) resection are common and significantly impact patient outcomes. Electrical impedance tomography (EIT) can be used to intraoperatively distinguish cancer from healthy tissue. The objective of this study was to develop and identify a fused EIT approach to accurately reconstruct a tumor boundary using multiple impedance measurements recorded with a handheld electrical impedance probe. Approach: Simulations and noise analyses were used to investigate the best reconstruction method for fused EIT, and a measured phantom experiment was conducted to evaluate the EIT reconstructions. Main results: Fused approaches successfully and accurately recovered the conductivity boundary location from multiple probe sites, increasing boundary localization to within 0.5 mm of the true boundary for both simulated and experimental results. Simulations and noise analysis revealed that all reconstruction methods maintain strong classification accuracy with added noise on a half-plane boundary scenario and fused difference EIT is the most robust to all types of added noise, due to the high data redundancy in the reconstruction formulation. Significance: The measured multi-site probe experiments represent important steps in the development of an approach that can combine EIT from multiple locations to increase boundary localization accuracy. Overall, this result is a step towards a clinically deployable impedance imaging approach to scanning the entire tumor boundary, which could significantly help to improve surgical outcomes for OSCC.

</details>


### [202] [Streamable Neural Video Compression: A Mixed Precision Approach for Cross-Platform Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00483)
*Kasidis Arunruangsirilert,Heming Sun,Jiro Katto*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流式客户端-服务器神经视频编解码(NVC)架构，通过混合精度(FP16/FP32)策略解决跨GPU平台浮点不确定性导致的熵编码器失步崩溃问题，在12块GPU上验证了跨代可解码性，并评估了Wi-Fi 6、5G NR和Starlink D2C等真实网络场景下的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经视频编解码器(NVC)在带宽受限环境（如5G蜂窝网络和卫星D2C链路）中具有卓越的率失真性能，但部署受限于跨平台浮点非确定性导致算术熵编码器跨GPU架构失步崩溃。现有INT8量化方法严重降低压缩效率，INT16方法则因绕过硬件加速造成计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种混合精度(FP16/FP32)策略：P帧使用硬件加速的FP16以保障实时吞吐量，I帧及周期性特征适配器重置强制使用IEEE-754兼容的FP32，确保关键边界处的确定性同步。

Result: 在覆盖四代架构的12块GPU上进行了跨编解码评估，成功消除了代内碎片化问题，显著扩大了跨芯片互操作性，实现了最新架构在1080p下的无缝跨代可解码性，且压缩效率影响可忽略。同时在实际网络（Wi-Fi 6、5G NR FDD/TDD、Starlink D2C）中验证了端到端延迟的实用可行性。

Conclusion: 混合精度策略为NVC在实际流媒体应用中的跨平台部署提供了可行的解决方案，平衡了确定性、实时性能和压缩效率，同时揭示了非地面网络(NTN)中流式学习视频压缩的独特挑战。

Abstract: Neural Video Codecs (NVCs) offer unprecedented rate-distortion performance, making them highly attractive for bandwidth-constrained environments like 5G cellular networks and emerging satellite direct-to-cell (D2C) links. However, deploying NVCs in real-world streaming applications is severely hindered by cross-platform floating-point non-determinism, which causes arithmetic entropy coders to desynchronize and crash across different GPU architectures. While recent integer-based quantization methods address this, they incur either massive degradation in compression efficiency (INT8) or severe computational bottlenecks by bypassing hardware acceleration (INT16). In this paper, we propose a streamable, client-server NVC architecture featuring a novel Mixed Precision (FP16/FP32) strategy. By strategically executing P-frames in hardware-accelerated FP16 for real-time throughput, while forcing I-frames and periodic feature-adapter resets to IEEE-754 compliant FP32, we guarantee deterministic synchronization at critical boundaries. Through extensive cross-encode/decode evaluations across 12 GPUs spanning four architectural generations, we demonstrate that our approach successfully eliminates intra-generation fragmentation and substantially broadens cross-die interoperability, achieving seamless cross-generation decodability for recent architectures at 1080p. Crucially, this is achieved with a negligible impact on compression efficiency. Furthermore, we evaluate the system's end-to-end latency across diverse real-world networks, including Wi-Fi 6, 5G NR (FDD/TDD), and Starlink D2C, proving the practical viability of streamable learned video compression while highlighting unique challenges in Non-Terrestrial Networks.

</details>


### [203] [Spinal-Multiple-Myeloma-SEG: A Dual-Energy CT Dataset Extended with Trabecular Bone Segmentation of Lumbar Vertebrae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01482)
*Michal Nohel,Vlastimil Valek,Katerina Krejci,Roman Jakubicek,Marek Dostal,Jiri Chmelik*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Spinal-Multiple-Myeloma-SEG数据集的扩展版本（Version 2），在原有双能CT影像数据基础上，增加了腰椎小梁骨隔室的专家验证分割标注，支持骨髓瘤相关的骨微结构体素级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性骨髓瘤常累及脊柱椎体，但缺乏针对椎体骨小梁隔室的精细分割标注数据，限制了对骨微结构进行定量分析和深度学习模型开发的研究。

Method: 数据集包含72例双能CT检查（67名成人患者），提供常规CT、虚拟单能影像和钙抑制重建图像及临床元数据。初始分割掩膜由预训练的nnU-Net模型生成，经专家手动修正和放射学质量控制确保解剖一致性。

Result: 发布了包含腰椎小梁骨分割标注的扩展数据集，以DICOM格式提供影像数据，以NIfTI和DICOM-SEG格式提供分割掩膜，通过TCIA和Zenodo双平台公开发布。

Conclusion: 该数据集支持小梁骨分割、骨密度定量分析以及多种深度学习任务，适用于病理性和非病理性骨骼的多模态学习研究，为骨髓瘤骨结构分析提供了标准化资源。

Abstract: We present an extension of the publicly available \textit{Spinal-Multiple-Myeloma-SEG} dataset, a dual-energy CT imaging resource for multiple myeloma research. The purpose of this dataset is to enable voxel-wise analysis of vertebral bone microstructure by adding expert-validated segmentation of the trabecular compartment of lumbar vertebrae. The dataset consists of 72 dual-energy CT examinations from 67 adult patients (mean age 66 years, range 48--85; 36\% female), acquired retrospectively using a dual-layer dual-energy CT system. It includes conventional CT, virtual monoenergetic images, and calcium-suppressed reconstructions, along with structured clinical metadata. The data are provided in DICOM format, while segmentation masks are available in both NIfTI and DICOM-SEG formats. The primary intended applications include trabecular bone segmentation, quantitative bone mineral density-related analysis, and development of deep learning models for vertebral anatomy and disease-affected bone structures in multiple myeloma. The dataset supports both segmentation and multimodal learning tasks in pathological and non-pathological bone. Initial trabecular segmentation masks were generated using a pretrained nnU-Net model and subsequently refined through manual expert correction and radiological quality control, ensuring anatomical consistency. The original dataset is publicly available via TCIA (\href{https://doi.org/10.7937/k4qv-hh78}{https://doi.org/10.7937/k4qv-hh78}), while the trabecular segmentation extension (Version 2) is released through Zenodo (\href{https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21628232}{https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21628232}) under the CC BY 4.0 license. The Zenodo release provides immediate public access to the segmentation masks and will be additionally incorporated into the official TCIA collection after completion of the curation process.

</details>


### [204] [Hadamard-Domain Model Quantization for Learned Image Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01653)
*Junqi Shi,Chongzhi Wang,Yiwen He,Ming Lu,Zhan Ma*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出HaTQ（Hadamard变换域量化）方法，通过正交Hadamard重参数化来重新分配权重和激活响应，使均匀INT8量化在学习的图像编码（LIC）中表现更好，支持PTQ和QAT，且兼容纯整数推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀INT8量化在LIC部署中很有吸引力，但由于重尾张量和通道间大变异，其率失真性能常受限。现有方法主要通过混合精度或非均匀码本来适配量化器，需要更有效的解决方案。
