Extensive experiments demonstrate that CADIR achieves higher geometric fidelity and execution reliability than existing CAD representations, that construction-graph retrieval further improves model generation quality, and that cross-backend editing enables reliable model reconstruction and post-reconstruction editing across multiple CAD environments.

</details>


### [272] [Modeling Social Dynamics with an LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00929)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Kathleen M. Carley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用GhostField架构（一种混合LLM智能体与基于代理的网络动态模型）构建AuraSight场景，模拟了314,244个异构网络社会智能体与人类参与者在30天内围绕虚构国际歌曲创作比赛交换529,327条消息的过程，并从四个分析层（自我中心网络拓扑、语义网络演化、协调动态和影响力动态）考察涌现的社会动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索社会动态如何从个体网络和话语互动中聚合涌现，以及生成式社会模拟是否能够复现真实的社会动态模式。

Method: 采用GhostField架构（混合LLM启用的智能体与网络动态模型，即LAND模型）构建AuraSight社会模拟场景，包含314,244个异构智能体和人类参与者，在30天内交换529,327条消息，并从四个分析层（ego网络拓扑、语义网络演化、协调动态、影响力动态）进行方法论考察。

Result: 结果表明生成的社交模拟确实能够产生社会动态，且协调与影响力的动态并非来自个体智能体，而是源于网络拓扑与叙事交换之间的递归互动。

Conclusion: 社会动态的涌现本质上是网络拓扑与叙事交换递归交互的结果，而非个体行为者的简单加总，这一发现对生成式社会模拟研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Social dynamics encode the process in which individual network and discourse interactions aggregate into collective influence, narrative dominance and coordinate behavior. This paper uses the the GhostField architecture, a hybrid LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) model as a social simulation framework to build the AuraSight scenario. In the AuraSight scenario, 314,244 heterogeneous cyber social agents and human actors exchange 529,327 messages over 30 days surrounding a fictional international song-writing contest. We methodologically examine emergent social dynamics across four analytical layers: ego-network topology, semantic network evolution, coordination dynamics and influence dynamics. Our results show how generated social simulations do also produce social dynamics, and how the dynamics of coordination and influence emerge not from individual agents but from the recursive interaction between network topology and narrative exchange.

</details>


### [273] [PMMC: Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation for Long-Term LVLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00962)
*Jingyu Sun,Yan Lin,Yuyang Xue,Yifan Wang,Zhengtao Yao,Rui Qian,Zefeng Xu,Jiachen Li,Xianyang Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Jingyuan Sun,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出前瞻性多模态记忆编译（PMMC）框架，通过将记忆推理过程从查询时转移到记忆固结时，使用Questioner预测未来问题、Planner编译条件化多模态记忆程序、Doubter验证证据路径，构建结构化问题库以提升查询效率和答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体记忆系统要么将视觉经验简化为文本摘要，要么依赖静态的检索-推理流水线，导致查询时效率低下，且在需要图像-文本绑定、时间更新或视觉细节的问题上表现脆弱。

Method: PMMC框架包含三个核心组件：Questioner负责预测未来可能的问题候选；Planner编译问题条件化的多模态记忆程序；Doubter验证计划的证据路径是否能支持预测答案。经过验证的问题-程序对形成结构化问题库，用于高效的查询时路由和证据检索。

Result: 在多模态长期记忆基准上的实验表明，该方法提高了答案质量和视觉证据召回率，同时降低了查询时的token和延迟成本。消融实验分析了自我反馈、动态规划、原始图像访问和问题库覆盖率的影响。

Conclusion: 将记忆推理过程前置到记忆固结阶段，通过前瞻性编译构建结构化问题库，可以有效兼顾回答质量与查询效率，是长时多模态记忆系统的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LVLM agents to maintain consistency and integrate information across extended multimodal interactions. Existing agent memory systems, however, often reduce visual experiences into textual summaries or rely on static retrieve-then-reason pipelines, which are inefficient at query time and brittle when questions require image-text binding, temporal updates, or visual details. We propose Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation, a framework that shifts part of the memory reasoning process from query time to memory consolidation time. Given accumulated multimodal interactions, a Questioner predicts future question candidates, a Planner compiles question-conditioned multimodal memory programs, and a Doubter verifies whether the planned evidence path can support the predicted answer. The verified question-program pairs form a structured question bank for efficient query-time routing and evidence retrieval. Experiments on multimodal long-term memory benchmarks show that our method improves answer quality and visual evidence recall while reducing query-time token and latency costs. Extensive ablations analyze the effects of self-feedback, dynamic planning, raw-image access, and question bank coverage.

</details>


### [274] [TrajWiki: Source-Grounded Memory Trajectories for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00967)
*Jingyu Sun,Yuyang Xue,Mingyang Li,Zhengtao Yao,Jiachen Li,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Haozhe Liu,Fangying Wang,Magdalene Katharina Montgomery,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajWiki提出了一种基于轨迹的对话代理记忆框架，将每条记忆表示为带来源溯源的演化轨迹，通过不可变的事件快照和ADD/REVISE/DEPRECATE等声明级操作来管理，并引入Memory Wiki中间层将对话历史增量编译为结构化的互链wiki页面，实现分层检索和证据溯源的答案生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强代理通常将记忆存储为孤立记录或可覆盖状态，难以追踪信息的来源、演化、冲突和过时过程，导致长时对话中缺乏可追溯、可更新且诊断透明的外部记忆。

Method: TrajWiki将每条记忆表示为来源可溯源的演化轨迹，通过不可变事件快照和声明级操作（ADD、REVISE、DEPRECATE）维护；引入Memory Wiki作为持久中间层，增量编译对话历史为结构化互链wiki页面；推理时采用分层路由，从wiki页面到记忆轨迹、快照和源消息进行证据检索和答案生成。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MedMT数据集上的实验表明，TrajWiki在开源和闭源LLM骨干上都提升了长时对话性能，同时提供了更强的可解释性和对记忆演化、检索失败及答案生成的诊断可见性。

Conclusion: TrajWiki通过轨迹化记忆表示和wiki化中间层有效解决了长时对话代理的记忆管理问题，兼顾了性能提升与可诊断性，为长时对话代理的记忆设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong capabilities in generating coherent and contextually appropriate responses, yet robust long-horizon dialogue remains limited by the lack of external memory that is traceable, updatable, and diagnostically transparent. Existing memory-augmented agents often store memories as isolated records or overwritable states, making it difficult to preserve how information originates, evolves, conflicts, or becomes obsolete over time. We propose TrajWiki, a trajectory-based memory framework for long-horizon conversational agents. Instead of treating memory as static entries, TrajWiki represents each memory as a source-grounded evolution trajectory, maintained through immutable episodic snapshots and claim-level operations such as ADD, REVISE, and DEPRECATE. To reduce fragmentation and retrieval cost, TrajWiki further introduces Memory Wiki, a persistent intermediate layer that incrementally compiles dialogue history into structured and interlinked wiki pages capturing salient entities, events, quantities, topics, and conflicts. At inference time, queries are routed hierarchically from relevant wiki pages to linked memory trajectories, then to corresponding snapshots and source messages for evidence-grounded answer synthesis. Experiments on LoCoMo and MedMT show that TrajWiki improves long-horizon dialogue performance across both open-source and closed-source LLM backbones, while providing greater interpretability and diagnostic visibility into memory evolution, retrieval failures, and answer generation.

</details>


### [275] [PROGRESS: Coverage-guided RL to Train Search-augmented LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00969)
*Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Aounon Kumar,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Wenbo Li,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PROGRESS方法，利用教师模型引导的覆盖率奖励来显式塑造搜索增强型LLM代理的查询分解行为，以解决现有RL训练中仅依赖结果级奖励、缺乏对搜索行为监督的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索增强型LLM代理通过强化学习训练提升推理能力，但主要依赖结果级奖励，对搜索行为的监督不足，且忽略了代理正确分解复杂查询的能力。需要更细粒度的引导来改善查询分解决策。

Method: 采用教师引导的覆盖率奖励（teacher-guided coverage reward）来显式塑造策略模型的分解查询生成。训练中，冻结的教师模型将复杂查询分解为必要的搜索查询，用于引导策略模型的搜索行为。该方法集成到R1风格的训练框架中，在不依赖密集过程级监督的情况下提供轻量级的查询分解决策引导。

Result: 实验表明，覆盖率引导的RL提升了整体任务性能，验证了显式监督智能体LLM中搜索策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式监督搜索策略（特别是查询分解）对于搜索增强型智能体LLM的性能提升至关重要，PROGRESS通过轻量级的教师引导覆盖率奖励有效实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Existing search-augmented LLM agents are trained using Reinforcement Learning to boost its reasoning capabilities. However, these approaches primarily rely on outcome-level rewards, which provide little supervision over search behavior and overlook agent's ability to decompose complex queries properly. To mitigate this issue, we propose PROGRESS which utilizes teacher-guided coverage reward to explicitly shape decomposed query generation of the policy model. During training, frozen teacher models are used to decompose complex queries into essential search queries. These essential search queries are utilized to guide the search behavior of the policy model. Integrated into an R1-style training framework, our approach provides lightweight guidance over query decomposition decisions without dense process-level supervision. Experiments show that coverage-guided RL improves overall task performance, highlighting the importance of explicitly supervising search strategies in agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [276] [Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00974)
*Aounon Kumar,Sudipta Paul,Vivek Kulkarni,Vijay Srinivasan,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GRT（Guided Retrieval Training）的新方法，通过在强化学习训练期间利用ground truth信息限制检索过程，为搜索代理提供更强的学习信号，解决了大语言模型在多跳问答任务中检索能力训练时奖励稀疏的问题，并在多种QA任务上取得了超过40%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂多跳问答任务中有效使用搜索引擎仍面临重大挑战，需要将问题分解为子查询、检索相关信息并综合多源答案，早期检索不佳会导致级联错误。虽然强化学习在改善LLM搜索能力方面有潜力，但稀疏奖励问题严重阻碍了模型的有效学习。

Method: 提出Guided Retrieval Training (GRT)方法，在强化学习训练阶段使用ground truth信息限制检索过程，将模型聚焦于一组精选的相关文档集，从而提供更强的学习信号，缓解稀疏奖励问题，提升模型生成准确子查询和综合正确答案的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明GRT在多种问答任务上相比现有方法（如Search-R1）取得了一致的性能提升。特别在多跳问答任务中实现了超过40%的性能改进，同时在更少的训练步骤下获得了更好的问答性能，提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: GRT通过引导检索训练有效解决了强化学习在LLM搜索能力训练中的稀疏奖励问题，显著提升了多跳问答任务的性能并提高了训练效率，为LLM搜索代理的训练提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: The effective use of search engines by large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, particularly in complex, multi-hop question-answering (MHQA) tasks. These tasks require the model to decompose questions into subqueries, retrieve relevant information, and synthesize answers from multiple sources, often leading to cascading errors due to poor retrieval in early stages. Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in improving LLMs' search capabilities, but it often suffers from sparse rewards during training, hindering the model's ability to learn effectively. To address these challenges, we introduce Guided Retrieval Training (GRT), a novel method that improves the performance of a search agent by restricting the retrieval process during RL training using ground truth information. By focusing on a curated set of relevant documents, GRT provides the model with a stronger learning signal, mitigating the problem of sparse rewards and improving its ability to generate accurate subqueries and synthesize correct answers. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRT achieves consistent performance improvements over existing methods, such as Search-R1, across a wide range of question-answering (QA) tasks. Notably, GRT excels in MHQA tasks, achieving over 40% improvements in performance. Additionally, GRT enhances training efficiency by achieving better QA performance with fewer training steps.

</details>


### [277] [Passing Coarse Marginal Checks Can Be Cheap: Persona Mixtures and Imprecise Treatment-Response Estimates in an LLM Persona Panel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00979)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了由16个轻量级人格条件化GPT-4.1配置组成的固定面板在重复策略博弈中的表现。面板在四个重复博弈单元中的三个通过了预先注册的宽泛参考条件均值标准，唯一未通过项仅比下参考界低0.011。研究揭示提示变异性高度依赖不确定性假设，处理效应改变了延续过程及其文本表征，且通过措辞和标签操作可实现对输出的表征控制。外部审查发现了多重缺陷，p13结果被视为复制目标而非确证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用作合成研究参与者，但通常仅通过边际响应是否类似人类数据来验证。作者旨在严格评估一个固定人格条件化模型面板在重复战略博弈中的行为，检验其是否满足预先注册的标准，并深入分析变异性来源、处理效应估计的可靠性以及结果的外部有效性。

Method: 使用固定面板的16个轻量级人格条件化GPT-4.1配置在重复战略博弈中进行实验。采用预注册的宽泛参考条件均值标准进行验证；使用对称Dirichlet敏感性分析（Jeffreys alpha=0.5和alpha=1）和有限机会plug-in估计来量化提示间变异性；进行联合延续概率对比分析，并设置保守的95%同时置信区间；通过措辞和位置操作及标签冲突操作来测试表征控制能力。

Result: 面板在四个重复博弈单元中的三个通过预注册标准，唯一未通过项仅低于下界0.011。变异强烈受提示索引影响，但具体比例取决于不确定性假设（63%-71% vs 47%-53% vs 85%-96%）。延续概率对比分别为+0.083和+0.078，95%区间均包含0。措辞操作将合作行为从0/40提升至37/40。外部审查发现家族误差、依赖、构造和边界不确定性等缺陷。

Conclusion: 注册的边际标准可以通过，但无需精确估计处理响应对象。p13结果不应被视为确证发现，而是复制目标。研究结果仅针对一个固定的模型-提示面板，不能确立人类可替代性。该研究强调了在评估LLM作为研究参与者时需要更严格的方法学和统计学框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as synthetic research participants and are often validated by whether their marginal responses resemble human data. We study a fixed panel of sixteen lightweight persona-conditioned GPT-4.1 configurations in repeated strategic games. The panel met preregistered broad-reference condition-mean criteria in three of four repeated-game cells; the sole miss was 0.011 below the lower reference bound. Variation was strongly prompt-indexed, but its share depended on uncertainty assumptions: fixed-panel symmetric-Dirichlet sensitivities produced median between-prompt shares of 63%-71% under Jeffreys alpha=0.5 and 47%-53% under alpha=1, while finite-opportunity plug-in estimates were 85%-96%. Aggregate continuation-probability contrasts were +0.083 and +0.078, with conservative simultaneous 95% intervals [-0.171, +0.330] and [-0.181, +0.330]. The treatment jointly changed the continuation process and its textual representation. A separate wording-and-position operation shifted cooperation from 0/40 to 37/40 in the bare configuration, and a label conflict also revealed representation control. The original persona-level p13 result was not prospectively family-controlled, while a post-adjudication exact gate was structurally underpowered; p13 is therefore a replication target rather than a finding. External review exposed family-error, dependence, construct, and boundary-uncertainty defects, and zero-call reanalysis changed the interpretation without rewriting the historical record. The registered marginal criteria could be passed without precisely estimating the treatment-response object. A public capsule verifies 4,916 confirmatory Phase 3-5 runs with no live model calls. The results concern one fixed model-prompt panel and do not establish human substitutability.

</details>


### [278] [Auditing Discovery Claims: A Two-Sided Criterion for Agentic Science, with the Negative Side Decidable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00981)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对AI科学系统的双侧审计框架，其核心是区分"真正的能力提升"与"搜索、验证器或评价器变化带来的虚假增益"。通过伪结自由预言机的正式限制来界定验证器范围，并在独立裁决者下对比自研系统与六个前沿模型，发现后者在更少计算下表现更优，但审计同时揭示了自研系统84%的效应来自随机序列即可解决的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的能力声称依据（基准提升、描述长度阈值或p值）无法区分真实能力提升与额外搜索、验证器变化或对不可靠评价器的适应，因此需要更严格的审计方法。

Method: 构建"两侧审计"：负面侧利用形式化事实（伪结自由预言机无法表示交叉碱基对）界定了验证器范围；正面侧引入独立裁决者，对比算子设计、搜索机制和预言机调用次数，并测试七个候选机制以确认可迁移性。

Result: 一个发明算子在对3个预测器优化时只有1/60存活；独立预测器确认其设计2个，而最小自由能求解器确认26个（p=8e-7）。两个前线模型在外部裁决者下以0.293对0.095获胜（p=5e-5），且节省4.6-10倍预言机调用。但审计也显示匹配的无目标搜索为精确零，84%效果来自随机序列已可解决的目标。

Conclusion: 审计达到前两个层级（外部裁决者差异、计算效率），但未达到第三层级（机制识别与可迁移性）。自研系统的能力声称在严格审计下大幅缩水，真实的能力提升证据仍然薄弱。

Abstract: When a self-improving AI-for-science system claims a new capability, the evidence is usually a benchmark delta, a description-length gate, or a p-value. None separates a real gain from extra search, from a changed verifier, or from adaptation to a fallible oracle. We build a two-sided audit whose negative side is a formal fact: a pseudoknot-free oracle provably cannot represent a crossing base pair, so the prior verifier's range is bounded exactly, offline, before any run. "New" is relative to the agent's prior self, never to the base model. First, how far a single fallible oracle can inflate a capability claim. An invented, solver-free operator solves 43/60 crossing RNA targets under the predictor it optimizes, above a context-free floor of 0/60; under three predictors, 1/60 survives. Paired on the same 43 targets, a predictor the operator never saw confirms 2 of its designs against 26 for a minimum-free-energy solver (p = 8e-7). No statistic computed from the system and its own oracle sees that gap. Second, agent-written procedures can beat a human-written one under a judge no objective can flatter, at a fraction of the compute. Of six frontier models, the two whose operators ran without timeouts carry over at 0.293 against our 0.095 (n = 951 paired units, target-clustered [+0.108, +0.297], p = 5e-5) while spending 4.6-10x fewer oracle calls. Three rungs: difference under an outside adjudicator (reached), not bought with compute (reached, both directions), mechanism identified and transferable (not reached; seven candidates tested, none moves the statistic). The ceiling is the panel itself: its three predictors share nearest-neighbour thermodynamic parameters, two agreeing at kappa = 0.673. The audit is as unsparing about our own system: matched undirected search is an exact zero, and a search-free probe puts 84% of our headline effect on targets a random sequence already solves.

</details>


### [279] [SCHEDBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Constraint Faithfulness in Natural-Language Combinatorial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00991)
*Shrenil Shaun Sharma,Avi Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCHEDBench，一个自然语言基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在表面形式变化下对组合调度约束忠实度的表现。该基准包含1,132个调度问题实例，涵盖作业车间调度、资源受限项目调度、护士排班和课程时间表等多个领域。实验发现，即使语义等价但表达形式不同的调度问题，LLM的生成结果也不可靠地一致，表面形式变化会降低解的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛应用于自然语言形式的调度问题求解，但其对语义等价的表面形式变化是否保持约束忠实度尚不明确。现有基准未系统评估LLM在约束可行性方面对NL表面形式变化的鲁棒性，因此需要构建一个专门的基准来量化这一问题。

Method: 作者构建了SCHEDBench基准，基于规范的调度实例和求解器推导的可行性/最优性作为金标准。实例通过领域特定模板、主题实体、词汇句法模板改写和约束级表面形式变化生成多种NL表述。评估了13个前沿和开源LLM，分析其在语义等价但表达不同的调度问题上生成调度的约束满足情况，并测试了约束重排序等多个变化轴对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，所有测试的LLM均无法在语义等价的调度问题表述上保持可靠的输出一致性。表面形式变化显著降低了生成调度的可行性，并在匹配实例上引发高于噪声水平的硬约束违反偏移。在测试的各个变化轴中，约束重排序表现出最明显的灵敏度。

Conclusion: SCHEDBench揭示了LLM在组合调度问题中对自然语言表面形式变化存在显著的不稳定性，说明当前模型在调度约束理解和忠实执行方面仍有重要局限。这为未来提升LLM在调度任务上的鲁棒性和可靠性提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: This paper introduces SCHEDBench, a natural-language benchmark for evaluating combinatorial scheduling constraint faithfulness under surface-form variation. Grounded in canonical scheduling instances and solver-derived feasibility and optimality, SCHEDBench assesses whether large language models (LLMs) generate schedules with the same constraint-feasible behavior across varied natural-language (NL) surface forms. SCHEDBench spans 1,132 instances across job-shop scheduling problems (JSP), single and multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling problems (RCPSP), nurse rostering/scheduling, and curriculum timetabling problems of varying difficulty. Instances are templated into natural language problems using domain-specific templates, themed entities, lexical-syntactic template rephrasing, and constraint-level surface-form variation, with reference solutions verified for feasibility and objective optimality. Across thirteen frontier and open-weight LLMs, we find that models are not reliably invariant to semantically equivalent renderings of the same scheduling problem. Surface-form variation reduces feasibility and induces above-noise shifts in per-instance hard-constraint violations on matched instances. Among the tested isolated axes, constraint reordering yields the clearest above-noise sensitivity.

</details>


### [280] [Judging Is Not Enumerating: Silent Omissions in LLM-Authored Acceptable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01000)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Peiji Long,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型作为"出题人"（而非"答题人"）的能力，发现其在一次性贪心生成测试集和评估标准时存在系统性缺陷：模型擅长判断候选是否属于某个集合，但不擅长自主构造该集合本身。核心问题是遗漏（omission）而非过度包含，且遗漏难以审查。通过重写出题结果而非直接使用，可大幅提升产出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的角色正从"被评估者"转变为"评估者"——它们编写测试套件、答案键、评分标准甚至奖励函数来定义其他系统的正确性。然而，这种"出题"角色下的能力尚未被严格测量，尤其是在实际部署中常用的一次性贪心生成（无测试时推理）协议下。

Method: 跨四个参考构建（两个有完整有限真值、一个硬化的可执行参考HumanEval+/MBPP+、一个显式不完整的词汇参考WordNet），测量模型作为出题者与判断者的能力差异。设计了对照实验（输出谓词vs输出外延）、遗漏/过度包含植入检测实验，并对43,227项生产部署数据及RLVR（强化学习验证）场景下的影响进行了量化分析。最后比较了"门控"与"修复"两种补救策略。

Result: 模型判断能力显著优于出题能力（算法构造上差距+0.34到+0.29 F1，跨24倍参数范围不闭合）；可执行代码上，F1达0.74-0.90的模型只生成能接纳19-42% oracle正确解的套件。对照实验显示模型输出谓词时F1约0.99，说明失败源于无法"物化"规范所指示的区域而非知识缺失。最主要错误是遗漏，植入检测显示模型发现过度包含是遗漏的6-7倍。RLVR场景中出题质量导致1.9点准确率损失和18.5 WordNet相对损失。门控策略将误拒率降至5%以下但仅保留5-39%套件；修复策略将产出提升3.3-10.6倍。

Conclusion: 模型在"出题人"角色下的核心瓶颈不是规范能力（specification）而是物化能力（materialisation）——它们能定义正确的谓词，却无法将其完整实例化为具体的集合成员。遗漏型错误在本质上难以被审查发现，因此需要从架构上改变出题协议（如引入参考执行的回写修复），而非仅依赖事后审查。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly promoted from examinees to examiners: they write the test suites, answer keys, rubrics, and reward functions that define correctness for other systems. We measure the capability that role assumes and find it lacking under the protocol the role is usually deployed with, one-shot greedy authoring with no test-time reasoning. Across four reference constructions - two with complete finite truth, one with a hardened executable reference (HumanEval+/MBPP+), one with an explicitly incomplete lexical reference (WordNet) - models judge whether a candidate belongs far better than they author the set itself. On the incompleteness-proof algorithmic construction the gap is +0.34 to +0.29 F1 over a 24x parameter range and does not close; on executable code, models judging at F1 0.74-0.90 author suites admitting only 19-42% of oracle-correct solutions. A control locates the deficit: asked to emit the predicate rather than its extension, the same models reach F1 about 0.99. The failure is not missing knowledge or an inability to specify, but an inability to materialise the region a specification induces. The dominant error is omission, which resists audit: an over-inclusion is a token a reviewer can challenge, a missing member an absence whose discovery is the authoring problem itself. Models detect planted over-inclusions 6-7x more often than planted omissions, and a production deployment of 43,227 items fails omission-first at 10:1. Wired into RLVR, an authored key costs 1.9 points of accuracy against an exact oracle and 18.5 WordNet-relative (six paired seeds, p=0.031). Gating authored verifiers on a known-correct probe cuts false rejection from 58-92% to at most 5%, but keeps only 5-39% of suites. Repairing them instead, by rewriting each wrong expected value to what a reference execution returns, raises yield 3.3-10.6x across four author families.

</details>


### [281] [From AI Technical Debt to Agentic Technical Debt: A Systematic Mapping of Root Causes and Manifestations in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01001)
*Muhammad Tukur,Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo,Tao Chen,Nour Ali,Anis Zarrad,Marco Agus,Rick Kazman,Rami Bahsoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic技术债务（AgTD）概念，将传统AI技术债务映射到自主多智能体AI系统中，并通过理论驱动的转化方法论建立了首个系统性的AITD到智能体表现的映射框架，揭示了技术债务如何从软件工件扩展到智能体行为、协调机制和交互层面，同时探讨了其对AI信任、风险与安全管理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI技术债务（AITD）模型基于静态、组件级架构假设，无法捕获Agentic AI系统固有的动态性和涌现行为。随着自主推理、多智能体协作、工具编排等能力的兴起，需要一种新的技术债务框架来理解和管理这些系统中的负债累积与传播。

Method: 基于先前对31种AITD和七类根因的系统性范围综述，采用三种理论驱动的转化方法论：直接转化（direct transformation）、情境转化（contextual transformation）和表现扩展（manifestation expansion），将传统技术债务重构为Agentic AI环境中的债务表现。

Result: 首次建立了成熟AITD到智能体表现的系统性映射，展示了传统债务如何演变为系统级负债（如内存不一致、编排脆弱性、级联故障和不安全自主决策），并发现技术债务已超出软件工件范畴，扩展到智能体行为、协调机制以及智能体-工具-执行环境之间的交互。

Conclusion: AgTD可作为基础软件工程构念，本研究提供了转化框架、分类学和未来研究议程，用于管理自主多智能体AI系统中的技术债务，并强调了其在AI信任、治理、安全、运营韧性和可持续性方面的深远影响。

Abstract: The emergence of Agentic AI systems, characterized by autonomous reasoning, multi-agent collaboration, tool orchestration, adaptive decision-making, and persistent memory, represents a fundamental shift from traditional AI pipelines to dynamic software ecosystems. While AI Technical Debt (AITD) has been widely studied in machine learning and software engineering, existing models assume static, component-level architectures and fail to capture the dynamic and emergent behaviors of agentic environments. To address this gap, this paper introduces Agentic Technical Debt (AgTD), defined as technical debt that emerges, accumulates, propagates, and amplifies due to the autonomous and collaborative nature of Agentic AI systems. Building on our prior systematic scoping review of 31 AITDs across seven root-cause categories, we employ a theory-informed transformation methodology to reinterpret these debts in Agentic AI through direct transformation, contextual transformation, and manifestation expansion. We present the first systematic mapping of established AITDs to their agentic manifestations, showing how conventional debts evolve into system-level liabilities, including memory inconsistencies, orchestration fragility, cascading failures, and unsafe autonomous decision-making. Our findings show that technical debt extends beyond software artifacts to encompass agent behaviors, coordination mechanisms, and interactions among agents, tools, and execution environments. We further examine its implications for AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (AI TRiSM), highlighting impacts on trustworthiness, governance, security, operational resilience, and Sustainability Technical Debt. Overall, this work establishes AgTD as a foundational software engineering construct and provides a transformation framework, taxonomy, and research agenda for managing technical debt in autonomous multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [282] [Toward Fine-Grained Forgetting:Attribute Unlearning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01008)
*Junkai Lin,Junkai Chen,Siqi Hou,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性级别的多模态大模型（MLLM）机器遗忘任务，构建了覆盖长文本、数值和短文本目标，多种遗忘比例和多样问题类型的基准，并提出CLRP（Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection）轻量级无训练框架，通过激活修补定位因果中介层，再应用保留感知投影去除目标属性子空间，同时保留同一身份的无关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私导向的机器遗忘基准主要采用档案级别（profile-level）删除，但实际隐私请求往往更细粒度——模型应遗忘特定属性，同时保留同一身份的其他非敏感信息。这种属性级别遗忘在MLLM中尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出CLRP框架，包含两个关键步骤：(1) 因果定位（Causal Localization）：利用激活修补（activation patching）识别因果中介目标属性泄露的层；(2) 保留感知投影（Retain-Aware Projection）：在定位到的层上，去除目标属性子空间同时保留同一身份的视觉证据。该方法为训练无关的轻量级方案。

Result: 实验表明，已有方法在属性级别遗忘中存在不稳定的遗忘—保留权衡；CLRP在多个广泛使用的不同架构和参数规模的MLLM上均展现出有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 属性级MLLM遗忘是一项更精细化且实用的任务，目标属性与保留属性共享身份特定和视觉证据，使得选择性遗忘面临残留泄露或附带退化的挑战；CLRP提供了一种有效、轻量且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit strong vision--language capabilities but may also memorize and disclose sensitive information. Machine unlearning seeks to remove designated knowledge without retraining from scratch while preserving general utility. Existing privacy-oriented benchmarks primarily adopt profile-level deletion, whereas practical requests are often finer grained: a model should forget a specified attribute while retaining non-sensitive information about the same identity. We therefore introduce attribute-level MLLM unlearning as a finer-grained task and construct a benchmark spanning long-text, numeric, and short-text targets, multiple forget ratios, and diverse question types. Our evaluation reveals that target and retained attributes share identity-specific and visual evidence, making selective forgetting susceptible to residual leakage or collateral degradation; accordingly, existing methods exhibit unstable forgetting--retention trade-offs in this setting. To address this challenge, we propose Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection (CLRP), a lightweight training-free framework. CLRP uses activation patching to identify the layer that causally mediates target-attribute disclosure, then applies a retain-aware projection that removes the target-attribute subspace while preserving same-identity evidence. Experiments across multiple widely used MLLMs with distinct architectures and parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness of CLRP.

</details>


### [283] [FactorJEPA: Factorizing Monolithic Futures into Layout-Agent-Interaction Channels for Crowded and Chaotic Global South Urban Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01049)
*Kapil Wanaskar,Gaytri Jena,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DENSEWORLD，一个涵盖全球南方22个城市、1000小时视频的大规模稠密城市场景数据集，并在此基础上提出FactorJEPA，一种将世界结构（布局、实体、交互）作为预测基元的联合嵌入预测架构，以解决拥挤混乱场景下JEPA模型难以保持密集交互动力学的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA模型主要在低密度、结构化车道的场景中评估，难以应对全球南方城市中拥挤、混乱、异构、遮挡严重且存在快速社交协商的真实环境。

Method: 构建DENSEWORLD-115k数据集（1000小时视频，22个城市）；提出FactorJEPA架构，将未来预测分解为布局、实体和交互三个子空间，引入可见性门控并分离子空间以防止跨因子捷径学习。

Result: FactorJEPA在三个关键指标上优于现有方法：未来潜在准确性（Future-frame L1）、干预敏感预测（Causal L1）和视觉证据鲁棒性（Mask-ratio slope）；方法排名在2B和1B V-JEPA 2.1骨干网络上高度一致（rho=0.895-0.978）。

Conclusion: 将世界结构显式建模为预测基元能有效提升稠密、异构、部分可观测城市场景中的未来预测能力，并发布了数据集和模型检查点以促进该方向研究。

Abstract: World models have attracted significant attention for their ability to capture and predict the structure and dynamics of the physical world. In this emerging landscape, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer a particularly compelling direction.
  We study a largely unexplored regime: populous, crowded, and chaotic Global South urban environments, which we call DENSEWORLD. Unlike the lower-density, lane-structured settings that dominate existing evaluations, these scenes exhibit soft spatial boundaries, extreme agent heterogeneity, persistent occlusion, and rapid social negotiation under mixed traffic. We introduce the first large-scale dataset for this regime: 1,000 hours of drive-through, walk-through, and aerial video across 22 cities. Existing JEPA formulations struggle to preserve dense interaction dynamics under heterogeneity and partial observability.
  We introduce FactorJEPA, which makes world structure a first-class predictive primitive. Rather than encoding the future in a monolithic latent, it composes layout, entities, and interactions, using a visibility gate and separated subspaces to preserve partially observed agents and discourage cross-factor shortcuts. FactorJEPA improves (i) future-latent accuracy (Future-frame L1), (ii) intervention-sensitive prediction (Causal L1), and (iii) robustness to reduced visual evidence (Mask-ratio slope), while exposing (iv) a reproducible motion-information trade-off (Motion cosine). Method rankings replicate across 2B and 1B V-JEPA 2.1 backbones, with rho = 0.895 to 0.978.
  We publicly release the DENSEWORLD-115k dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/denseworld-115k) and the surgery-trained FactorJEPA checkpoints (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/factorjepa-outputs/tree/main/outputs/full/vjepa_2_1_vitg_1B/train/m09c_surgery_3stage_DI_diheavy_encoder).

</details>


### [284] [Don't Offer What Can't Be Done: Deterministic Executability Gating for LLM Skill Selection at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01050)
*Ortal Ashkenazi,Vitalii Kloz,Mykhailo Ulianchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Wix客服助手Helpmate的三阶段技能选择流水线：先通过语义匹配快速召回相关技能，再通过确定性可执行性门控过滤掉当前账户状态下无法执行的技能，最后由LLM决定是否调用剩余候选技能。生产环境分析显示该方案大幅减少了上下文开销并防止了不可执行技能对模型决策的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的LLM代理从大型技能库中选择技能时，仅靠语义相关性是不够的——一个技能可能在主题上匹配用户需求，但在当前账户状态下无法执行。这种不一致会导致模型选择不可执行的技能，影响用户体验和系统效率。

Method: 提出三阶段选择流水线：(1) 召回导向的语义匹配器，在不查看账户状态的情况下识别与十个技能领域相关的消息；(2) 确定性可执行性门控，通过检查内部硬停止条件过滤候选技能，该门控与技能本身评估相同的退出谓词；(3) LLM在剩余候选中决定是否调用某个技能。

Result: 在756.6K条用户消息（267.6K会话）的生产分析中，语义匹配保留了23.1%的消息；门控剔除了59.4%的技能-消息对，节省了228.8百万个技能描述token（占语义匹配后足迹的59.1%）；整体上下文减少了90.5%。反事实实验中，暴露全部十个技能时模型在7.8%的会话中选择了被生产阻断的技能，证明确定性门控能防止不可执行候选影响模型选择。

Conclusion: 确定性可执行性门控能有效防止非可执行技能候选干扰LLM的选择行为，同时大幅降低上下文开销，且不声称对下游工具执行或客户结果有直接影响。

Abstract: Production LLM agents that select from large skill libraries face a limitation that semantic relevance alone cannot resolve: a skill may match a user's topic yet be impossible to execute in the current account state. We present a deployed three-stage selection pipeline for Helpmate, Wix's customer-care assistant. First, a recall-oriented semantic matcher identifies messages related to a ten-skill domain family without consulting account state. Second, a deterministic executability gate removes candidates whose internal hard-stop conditions hold. Because the gate and the skill evaluate the same exit predicates, every blocked candidate would be unable to complete under the same account state, provided predicate parity is preserved and both checks observe fresh authoritative state. Finally, the LLM decides whether to invoke one of the remaining candidates.
  In a post-launch production analysis of 756.6K user messages across 267.6K conversations, semantic matching retained 174,927 messages (23.1%). Within this matched stream, the gate removed 1,039,462 of 1,749,270 skill-message pairs (59.4%), saving 228.8 million skill-description tokens -- 59.1% of the post-semantic skill-description footprint. Together, semantic matching and executability gating reduced skill-description context by 90.5% relative to exposing all ten skills to every message.
  To test whether this pruning affects model behavior rather than context size alone, we replayed a risk-enriched cohort of 1,000 conversations with all ten skills exposed. The model selected a production-blocked skill in 78 conversations (7.8%). This counterfactual result shows that deterministic gating prevents non-executable candidates from influencing model selection, while not claiming downstream tool execution or customer-outcome effects.

</details>


### [285] [Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01056)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CompressAgent，一个环境验证的代理控制上下文（ACC）压缩基准测试框架，通过对9个ACC、3个任务族、3个Qwen API模型、6个保留上下文预算和15,525次运行的系统评估，揭示了ACC压缩的非线性可靠性前沿：在75%保留率时压缩接近基线性能，但在35%及以下时各方法急剧分化，最低仅19.9%成功率。压缩失败主要表现为工具执行和动作解析错误，表明ACC压缩应被视为运行时可靠性问题而非简单的token缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示压缩评估无法反映工具使用语言代理在系统侧指令（工具、参数、策略、执行协议、恢复机制）压缩后的操作可靠性。随着ACC压缩可降低输入成本，需要对其压缩后的控制可靠性进行系统性评估，而非仅关注压缩比例或语义保持。

Method: 构建CompressAgent基准：包含9个独立构建的ACC、3个任务族、3个固定Qwen API模型标识符、6个保留上下文预算（75%至10%），共15,525次运行。在环境验证下比较通用重写、基于章节的压缩、义务感知压缩等方法在不同压缩率下的成功率，并进行失败类型分析。

Result: 75%保留率时通用重写和章节压缩分别达到92.7%和92.4%成功率，接近93.8%的全上下文基线；35%时章节压缩47.0%、义务感知39.0%、通用重写仅19.9%；25%至10%时执行协议变得脆弱。压缩失败主要体现为工具执行错误和动作解析错误，且可靠性在不同ACC间差异显著。

Conclusion: ACC压缩应从token减少问题重新定义为运行时可靠性问题，必须通过可执行结果进行评估。通用压缩器排名不具备普遍适用性，需要针对具体ACC进行资格认证（per-context qualification）。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents are governed not only by task prompts but also by persistent system-side instructions that specify tools, arguments, policies, execution protocols, and recovery. Compressing these agent control contexts (ACCs) can reduce input cost and context use, yet existing prompt-compression evaluations do not reveal whether the resulting control remains operationally reliable. We introduce CompressAgent, an environment-verified benchmark for ACC compression across nine independently constructed ACCs, three task families, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. We uncover a nonlinear, method-dependent reliability frontier. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression achieve 92.7% and 92.4% success, close to the 93.8% full-context baseline. Between 50% and 35%, methods diverge sharply; at 35%, section-based, obligation-aware, and generic rewriting achieve 47.0%, 39.0%, and 19.9%. At retained-context budgets from 25% to 10%, executable protocols become fragile. Reliability also varies substantially across ACCs, making universal compressor rankings inappropriate and motivating per-context qualification. Failure analysis shows that compression primarily surfaces as tool-execution and action-parsing errors. These findings recast ACC compression from token reduction into a runtime-reliability problem that must be evaluated through executable outcomes.

</details>


### [286] [Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01075)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出角色解耦注意力残差（RD-AttnRes），将查询/键的深度路由与值的深度路由分离，在120M和343M参数模型上均显著提升验证性能，表明注意力匹配与内容检索应从残差层级的不同深度读取信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Block Attention Residuals使用单一逐内容深度混合来构造Q、K、V的输入，但查询/键决定注意力匹配位置，而值决定内容检索，这两类决策在功能上不同，不应被强制耦合。

Method: RD-AttnRes是一种最小扩展：查询和键共享一条深度路由，值则学习独立的深度路由（仅增加每层一个模型宽度向量），同一残差源上进行解耦。在FineWeb-Edu上采用冻结配对预训练协议，对120M和343M参数模型各使用5个匹配种子，训练预算2.0B tokens。

Result: 在全部10组匹配对比中，RD-AttnRes均改善验证负对数似然，平均降低0.0301（120M）和0.0247（343M），对应困惑度分别降低2.97%和2.43%。早期预算控制实验排除了参数数量、重复路由执行或固定值路由的干扰，路由诊断显示Q/K与V深度分布持续分歧。

Conclusion: 在评估的训练范围内，注意力匹配和内容检索确实受益于对残差层级的不同读取，即深度路由解耦是有益的架构设计选择。

Abstract: Depth-routing residual architectures allow Transformer layers to retrieve earlier representations instead of inheriting only the immediately preceding state. Existing Block Attention Residuals, however, use a single content-dependent depth mixture to construct the inputs to queries, keys, and values. This design couples two functionally different decisions: queries and keys determine where attention matches, whereas values determine what content is retrieved. We therefore ask whether matching and content retrieval should be forced to read from the same depth. We introduce Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals (RD-AttnRes), a minimal extension that shares one depth route between queries and keys while learning an independent value route over the same residual sources. Tying the two routing queries exactly recovers the parent architecture, while decoupling them adds only one model-width vector per layer and introduces no additional token-to-token attention operation. We evaluate RD-AttnRes using a frozen, paired pretraining protocol on FineWeb-Edu with five matched seeds for both 120M- and 343M-parameter models and a 2.0B-token training budget. RD-AttnRes improves validation negative log-likelihood in all 10 matched comparisons. The mean reductions are 0.0301 and 0.0247, corresponding to perplexity reductions of 2.97 percent and 2.43 percent at 120M and 343M parameters, respectively. Early-budget controls indicate that neither the additional parameter count, duplicated routing execution, nor a fixed value route reproduces the improvement. Routing diagnostics further reveal persistent divergence between the query-key and value depth distributions. These results suggest that, within the evaluated training regime, attention matching and content retrieval benefit from distinct reads over the residual hierarchy.

</details>


### [287] [Inter-Residue Geometry Attention for Antibody-Specific Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01092)
*Chuanliu Fan,Nan Yu,Junjie Wu,Guohong Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LF3DRoPE（局部框架三维旋转位置编码），将折叠后的残基三维几何位移直接注入旋转注意力机制中，作为位置编码替代传统一维序列顺序，在AsEP基准上取得最优MCC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体特异性表位预测方法虽然利用PLM嵌入和结构编码器，但注意力内的位置机制仍主要依赖一维序列顺序。然而对于蛋白质而言，残基间的"偏移"不仅包含序列距离，还应包含折叠后的三维空间位移。作者提出疑问：折叠后的残基几何能否直接作为注意力本身的位置机制？

Method: 提出Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding（LF3DRoPE），将残基间位移在主链定义的局部框架中表达，并直接注入旋转注意力机制中。该设计保持了连续的方向几何信息，同时保证对全局SE(3)变换的不变性。

Result: 在AsEP基准上，LF3DRoPE在ratio和epitope-group两种划分下均达到最先进的MCC。消融实验和刚体变换测试表明局部三维几何提供了超越序列顺序注意力的信息，同时保持对任意全局坐标系的变换不变性。突变排序结果进一步表明LF3DRoPE能够捕捉抗原特异性结构兼容性。

Conclusion: LF3DRoPE验证了蛋白质折叠后的三维残基几何可以替代一维序列顺序作为注意力位置机制，为抗体特异性表位预测提供了一种兼顾几何信息与SE(3)不变性的新方案。

Abstract: Antibody-specific epitope prediction aims to identify which antigen residues are recognized by a given antibody, a task that depends on the three-dimensional complementarity between antibody CDRs and the antigen surface. Existing methods usually leverage PLM embeddings and inject structure through additional graph, surface, or point-cloud encoders, where the positional mechanism inside attention remains largely tied to one-dimensional sequence order. For proteins, the analogue of a token offset is not only sequence separation, but also the three-dimensional displacement between residues after folding. This raises a question, can folded residue geometry serve as the positional mechanism of attention itself? We propose Local-Frame 3D Rotary Position Encoding (LF3DRoPE), which expresses inter-residue displacements in backbone-defined local frames and injects them directly into rotary attention. This design preserves continuous directional geometry while ensuring invariance to global $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ transformations. On the AsEP benchmark, LF3DRoPE achieves state-of-the-art $\mathrm{MCC}$ on both ratio and epitope-group splits. Ablations and rigid transformation tests show that local three-dimensional geometry provides information beyond sequence-order attention while preserving invariance to arbitrary global coordinate systems. Mutation ranking results further indicate that LF3DRoPE captures antigen-specific structural compatibility.

</details>


### [288] [Fighting Fire with Fire: On the Feasibility of Protecting Exercises Against AI Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01112)
*Tobias Braun,Jonas Grebe,Louis Rethfeld,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用对抗性机器学习技术保护教育测评免受生成式AI辅助作弊的影响，通过对多模态选择题的视觉组件施加细微扰动，诱导AI求解器产生特定错误答案，从而形成统计指纹以识别盲目复制AI答案的学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的普及使学生可以将认知任务外包给越来越强大的AI助手，造成能力错觉并破坏独立推理能力的培养，亟需一种能对抗这种对AI的依赖、保护教育测评完整性的方法。

Method: 采用多模态选择题，对其视觉组件施加精心设计的细微对抗性扰动，诱导AI模型错误回答特定题目；通过可访问的替代模型优化扰动以产生一致的响应模式；使用统计假设检验对学生的答案模式进行检测，识别出盲目复制AI答案的学生。

Result: 在现实黑箱助手假设下，使用Claude、Gemini和ChatGPT三种主流多模态语言模型验证了该范式的可行性，证明对抗扰动能诱导一致的响应模式并能通过统计假设检验实现原理性检测，同时也揭示了该方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"利用机器自身的脆弱性对抗机器辅助推理"这一范式的前景与局限，为教育场景中检测AI辅助作弊提供了新思路。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI enables students to outsource cognitive effort to increasingly capable assistants, creating an illusion of competence while undermining the independent reasoning that education aims to cultivate. We investigate whether adversarial machine learning can be repurposed to protect educational exercises against such corrosive reliance. Our approach uses multimodal multiple-choice questions whose visual components can be protected with subtle visual perturbations that steer AI solvers toward designated incorrect answers. These responses form a statistical fingerprint: students who blindly copy a solver reproduce the induced answer pattern more frequently than genuine students. We study the feasibility of this paradigm under realistic black-box assistant assumptions using three of the most common state-of-the-art multimodal language models: Anthropic's Claude, Google's Gemini, and OpenAI's ChatGPT. By using accessible surrogate models, we optimize adversarial perturbations that induce consistent response patterns. Those patterns enable principled detection through statistical hypothesis testing. These findings establish both the promise and the limitations of fighting machine-assisted reasoning with the vulnerabilities of the machines themselves.

</details>


### [289] [MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01128)
*Yixin Zhang,Zhuohui Yao,Wenchi Cheng,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机辅助应急通信中的信息年龄（AoI）最小化问题，提出MA-HEAD-Net，一种自适应规则引导的多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过联合优化无人机轨迹、用户调度和检查点间隔选择，在动态应急通信场景中取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后场景中无人机建立的应急通信网络对信息时效性要求极高，过时数据会导致救援决策失效。现有的AoI优化方法在异构紧急服务、突发数据到达和有限块长传输等复杂条件下难以有效平衡长包传输与紧急短包响应。

Method: 采用马尔可夫调制泊松过程建模突发数据到达，利用有限块长理论刻画传输时长、数据包完成与AoI演化的耦合关系；提出微时隙嵌入调度机制和自适应检查点间隔选择；将无人机轨迹控制、用户调度和检查点间隔选择联合优化为多智能体决策问题，开发MA-HEAD-Net框架，将通信领域规则先验融入门控多头策略网络，并在多智能体近端策略优化下联合优化策略与门控组件。

Result: 仿真结果表明，MA-HEAD-Net相比代表性的多智能体深度强化学习基线提升了策略形成效率，并在动态无人机应急通信场景中实现了比基于学习的方法和启发式方法更低的AoI。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将通信领域规则先验与多智能体深度强化学习相结合可以有效提升无人机应急通信中信息新鲜度的优化性能，为时敏救援场景下的通信网络设计提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: In post-disaster scenarios, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are critical for establishing emergency communication networks. For time-critical rescue missions, information freshness is crucial because decisions based on outdated data may lead to ineffective control actions. This paper investigates age of information (AoI) minimization for UAV-assisted emergency communications with heterogeneous emergency services. We model bursty packet arrivals using a Markov-modulated Poisson process and adopt finite blocklength theory to capture the coupling among transmission duration, packet completion, and AoI evolution. To balance delay-tolerant long-packet transmission and urgent short-packet response, we propose a mini-slot-embedded scheduling mechanism with adaptive checkpoint-interval selection. We formulate the joint optimization of UAV trajectory control, user scheduling, and checkpoint-interval selection as a multi-agent decision problem, and develop MA-HEAD-Net, an adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework. MA-HEAD-Net incorporates communication-domain rule priors into a gated multi-head policy, where adaptive gates regulate the contributions of rule-prior and learned-policy logits for different subtasks. The policy and gating components are jointly optimized under multi-agent proximal policy optimization. Simulation results show that MA-HEAD-Net improves policy-formation efficiency compared with representative multi-agent deep reinforcement learning baselines and achieves lower AoI than both learning-based and heuristic methods in dynamic UAV-assisted emergency communication scenarios.

</details>


### [290] [PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01149)
*Xidong Yang,Xingyi Zhang,Wenhao Li,Wenyan Liu,Junjie Sheng,Yun Hua,Wei Yin,Tao Fang,Chuyun Shen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PATH-Bench基准，用于评估终身学习智能体在路径依赖场景下的迁移与保持能力，并基于基准发现提出了Selective Experience Use（SEU）代理框架，通过选择性过滤历史经验来减少遗忘并提升正向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习智能体基准很少考虑经验积累路径如何影响智能体迁移和保持的知识，缺乏对路径依赖效应的系统评估。

Method: PATH-Bench通过多模型上下文学习估计有向任务关系，构建探针中心的任务序列以控制帮助性和干扰性历史，反复评估探针任务以测量平均性能、正向迁移、反向迁移和遗忘；同时提出SEU代理框架，调控路径积累的经验对新任务的影响，过滤潜在干扰。

Result: 对八种代表性智能体在代码生成和工具使用任务上的评估表明：经验效用取决于经验表示方式与任务交互结构的联合作用；强迁移不等于强保持；后期经验可重塑早期获得的收益。SEU在大多数设置中一致减少遗忘并提升正向迁移。

Conclusion: PATH-Bench提供了受控的评估框架和设计更具选择性与鲁棒性的终身智能体的可操作指导。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly adapt through external learning states that store past interactions as retrievable memories or reusable skills, yet existing benchmarks rarely account for how the path of accumulated experience shapes what agents transfer and retain. In this work, we establish PATH-Bench, a benchmark for path-dependent evaluation of lifelong agents. PATH-Bench estimates directed task relationships via multi-model in-context learning, constructs probe-centered sequences with controlled helpful and interfering histories, and repeatedly evaluates probe tasks to measure average performance, forward transfer, backward transfer, and forgetting. We evaluate eight representative agents on single-turn code generation and multi-turn tool-use tasks under positive- and negative-dominant histories. Benchmark results show that experience utility depends jointly on how experience is represented and on the task's interaction structure, that strong transfer does not ensure retention, and that later experience can reshape gains acquired earlier in the learning path. Based on these findings, we propose Selective Experience Use (SEU), an agent harness that regulates how path-accumulated experience influences each new task, admitting helpful items while filtering out potential interference. SEU consistently reduces forgetting while improving forward transfer in the majority of settings. The PATH-Bench provides both a controlled evaluation framework and actionable guidance for designing more selective and robust lifelong agents.

</details>


### [291] [The Graph Language: How Knowledge Graphs Speak to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01175)
*Giuseppe Pirrò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRALAN是一种通过关系令牌将知识图谱直接映射到LLM语义空间的框架，使冻结的LLM能够理解和利用图结构信息，在复杂多跳推理问答任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理方面表现出色，但缺乏知识图谱提供的结构化知识支撑。然而，将知识图谱与LLM整合面临巨大挑战，现有方法难以在保持图结构完整性的同时充分利用LLM的推理能力。

Method: GRALAN提出了一种可训练的语言中介器（trainable language mediator），通过生成保留图结构的关系令牌（relational tokens），使任何冻结的LLM都能直接理解知识图谱的语义。在问答任务中，将问题重新框定为基于问题聚焦子图的实体分类任务。

Result: 实验表明GRALAN显著优于现有方法，特别是在复杂多跳推理任务上表现突出，展示了其在知识密集型应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRALAN建立了一种KG-LLM整合的新范式，既保持了图结构的保真度，又充分利用了LLM的推理能力，为知识密集型应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at reasoning but benefit from grounding provided by Knowledge Graphs (KGs). However, integrating these paradigms is challenging. We introduce GRALAN, which enables KGs to speak directly in the LLM's semantic space through relational tokens that preserve graph structure. GRALAN-s trainable language mediator generates structured tokens for any frozen LLM, creating a foundation for knowledge-intensive applications. We demonstrate its effectiveness in question-answering by re-framing the task as entity classification over question-focused subgraphs. Experiments show that GRALAN significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly on complex multi-hop reasoning tasks, establishing a new paradigm for KG-LLM integration that maintains structural fidelity while leveraging LLMs' reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [292] [Co-evolution of social reward and punishment under institutional interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01183)
*Van An Nguyen,Vuong Khang Huynh,Hoai Thuong Nguyen,Duc Tin Duong,An Nguyen Gia,Tat Kien Nguyen,Huu Loi Bui,My Nguyen Tra,Ho Nam Duong,Ba Thanh Phan,Thanh Vo,Dinh Anh Trung Hoang,Adeela Bashir,Zhao Song,Manh Hong Duong,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究同行激励与机构激励如何共同作用于社会困境中的合作演化，发现同行惩罚最能促进合作但同行奖励更有利于社会福利，机构奖励同行激励策略可显著提升合作与福利，而机构惩罚同行激励策略会瓦解去中心化机制并损害社会福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多单独考察同行激励或机构激励的作用，很少关注两者同时存在时的交互效应，缺乏对机构如何设计干预方案以平衡合作促进与社会福利最大化的系统性指导原则。

Method: 构建包含无条件合作者、背叛者、社会惩罚者和社会奖励者四种策略的囚徒困境模型，允许去中心化同行激励与中心化机构激励同时作用。在无限混合良好种群中用四策略复制者动力学分析均衡状态与演化流，在结构化种群中用方格晶格上的基于智能体模拟研究空间效应与网络互惠。

Result: 同行惩罚最能促进合作，同行奖励更有利于社会福利；机构奖励同行激励策略可显著改善合作与福利，但补贴无条件合作者效果甚微；在机构惩罚下，直接惩罚背叛者是唯一持续有效的政策，惩罚同行激励策略会瓦解去中心化激励、降低合作并损害社会福利。

Conclusion: 最大化合作并不一定等同于优化整体社会效益，研究为机构提供了平衡合作促进与福利最大化的设计原则。

Abstract: We investigate how peer and institutional incentives jointly shape the evolution of cooperation, social welfare, and enforcement efficiency in social dilemmas. In a Prisoners Dilemma with four strategies, unconditional cooperators (C), defectors (D), social punishers (SP), and social rewarders (SR), we allow decentralised peer incentives and centralised institutional incentives to act simultaneously, with the institution able to reward or punish any subset of strategies. In infinite well-mixed populations, we analyse the resulting four-strategy replicator dynamics, and in structured populations we use agent-based simulations on square lattices to study spatial effects and network reciprocity. Intervention schemes are evaluated by equilibrium states and evolutionary flow for infinite well-mixed populations, by cooperation levels and social welfare for structured populations, defined as aggregate population payoff net of institutional cost. We find that peer punishment most strongly promotes cooperation, whereas peer reward is more beneficial for social welfare. Institutionally rewarding peer incentive strategies substantially improves both cooperation and welfare, while subsidising unconditional cooperators has little impact. Under institutional punishment, directly penalising defectors is the only consistently effective policy; punishing peer incentive strategies dismantles decentralised incentives, reduces cooperation, and harms social welfare, showing that maximising cooperation does not necessarily optimise overall societal benefit. Our findings provide design principles for institutions seeking to balance cooperation promotion with welfare maximisation.

</details>


### [293] [Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01193)
*Phu Hoa Pham,Duy Minh Dao Sy,Trung Kiet Huynh,Phu Quy Nguyen Lam,Chi Nguyen Tran,Minh Trung Le,Phong Hao Le,Dinh Nam Nguyen,Thien Ky Nguyen Dong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体AI竞赛中的安全困境，使用重复游戏让两到五名LLM智能体在"缓慢安全发展"与"快速冒险发展"之间做出选择。作者在行为解释前设置了审计关卡（验证游戏引擎、规则记忆、状态追踪、收益计算和任务描述稳定性），发现强规则记忆可能与弱状态追踪和收益计算并存，且不同模型在动作序列、对手响应和比赛位置响应上存在巨大差异，表明多智能体仿真需要有效性检查和轨迹级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI开发竞赛中，公司面临安全发展的两难困境。现有研究常将LLM智能体的动作直接解释为"战略性"或"安全性"行为，但有效的动作并不代表智能体真正理解游戏。作者希望通过严格的审计流程来验证LLM智能体是否真正理解游戏规则和机制，避免对其行为做出过度解读。

Method: 方法包括：（1）首先验证游戏引擎的正确性；（2）测试规则记忆、状态追踪、收益计算能力，以及在不同但等价的任务描述下的稳定性；（3）将LLM动作序列与进化博弈论基准及已发表的人类数据进行比较；（4）探索不同模型、风险条件、角色设定和两到五名玩家竞赛中的差异；（5）在七个模型端点上进行测试。

Result: 审计发现：强规则记忆可与弱状态追踪和收益计算能力共存；提供经过验证的算术并改变响应表示方式会改变后续动作（即使游戏规则不变）；七个测试模型端点的聚合速率掩盖了动作序列、对手响应和比赛位置响应上的巨大差异；三到五名玩家竞赛的行为模式因模型而异，并非添加竞争者的单一效应。

Conclusion: 多智能体AI竞赛模拟在输出被描述为战略性、类人或安全感知之前，必须进行有效性检查和轨迹级分析。研究结果为探索性发现，仅适用于所测试的模型、提示和解码设置。

Abstract: An AI development race creates a multi-agent safety dilemma. Each company can develop slowly and safely, or move faster while taking a risk that may remove its final reward. We use this repeated game to study strategic safety behaviour among large language model (LLM) agents in races with two to five players. However, a valid action does not show that an agent understands the game. We therefore place an audit gate before behavioural interpretation. We first verify the game engine, then test rule recall, state tracking, payoff calculation, and stability under different but equivalent task descriptions. We then compare LLM action sequences with an evolutionary game-theory benchmark and published human data, and explore differences across models, risk conditions, personas, and two- to five-player races. The audit shows that strong rule recall can coexist with weak state tracking and expected-payoff calculation. Providing verified arithmetic and changing the response representation can also change later actions, even when the game rules stay fixed. Across seven tested model endpoints, aggregate rates hide large differences in action sequences, responses to opponents, and responses to race position. Patterns across the tested three- to five-player races are also model-specific rather than a single effect of adding competitors. These results show why multi-agent AI-race simulations need validity checks and trajectory-level analysis before their outputs are described as strategic, human-like, or safety-aware. Our findings are exploratory and apply only to the tested models, prompts, and decoding settings.

</details>


### [294] [Reputation-driven Cooperation in Lattice-based Decentralized Federated Learning through Evolutionary Game Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01197)
*Phuc Hoang Truong Huynh,Dung Tran Vinh,Khoa Duc Anh Lam,An Nghiem Nguyen Truong,Uyen Nha Tran Bui,Khang Nguyen Dinh,Bao Nguyen Le Gia,Minh Le Nguyen Nhat,Manh Hong Duong,The Anh Han,Thi Ai Thao Nguyen,and Le Hong Trang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于演化博弈论（EGT）的新型去中心化联邦学习（DFL）框架，在有限理性假设下模拟格子网络上的P2P交互，并引入基于声誉的奖惩机制抑制搭便车行为。实验表明该框架将平均准确率从约70%提升至82%，合作频率接近100%，精度方差从0.40降至0.002。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习缺乏中央协调者，容易受到机会主义行为（如搭便车）的威胁。现有基于演化博弈论的研究通常假设智能体完全理性且策略静态，与实际场景不符，亟需更符合现实的框架来分析策略演化并提升系统性能。

Method: 首先在格子网络结构上建模P2P交互，并采用有限理性假设；其次构造包含训练成本、通信开销和合作奖励的综合收益矩阵，并设计能刻画空间传播动态的策略更新规则；最后整合基于声誉的奖惩机制以有效威慑搭便车行为。

Result: 与基线相比，所提框架将平均准确率从约70%提升至82%，合作频率从低于5%提升至接近100%，精度方差从约0.40降至0.002，显著加速了均匀收敛并保证了系统稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的EGT框架能有效分析DFL中的策略演化，通过声誉机制显著提升系统合作水平、准确率和稳定性，为去中心化联邦学习中的机会主义行为治理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) has emerged as an optimal privacy-preserving solution; however, it remains vulnerable to opportunistic behaviors due to the absence of a central coordinator. While Evolutionary Game Theory (EGT) serves as a powerful framework for analyzing such behaviors, existing studies often assume that agents possess perfect rationality and maintain static strategies. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel EGT framework designed to analyze strategic evolution and enhance overall system performance. The primary contributions of this work are threefold: First, we model peer-to-peer (P2P) interactions on a lattice network structure under the assumption of bounded rationality. Second, we formulate a comprehensive payoff matrix incorporating training costs, communication overhead, and cooperative rewards, while tailoring a strategy update rule that captures spatial propagation dynamics. Third, we integrate a reputation-based reward-and-punishment mechanism to effectively deter free-riding behaviors. Simulation results demonstrate that the framework significantly outperforms the baseline. Specifically, it increases average accuracy from approximately 70% to 82%, elevates cooperation frequency to approach 100% (compared to below 5% in the baseline), and drops accuracy variance from around 0.40 to 0.002, thereby accelerating uniform convergence and ensuring system stability.

</details>


### [295] [Perspectives on Tsallis Statistics for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01223)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了Tsallis统计（q-统计）与人工智能的交叉领域，从数学基础（q-熵、q-指数、q-中心极限定理等）到AI应用（稀疏注意力、强化学习、生成模型、损失函数设计等），并指出深度学习中的重尾现象本质上是非广延统计特征，主张将q参数作为可学习的归纳偏置而非固定超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsallis统计已在现代AI中广泛出现（如sparsemax、α-entmax、最大熵强化学习等），但缺乏一个统一的结构化视角来理解其数学核心与应用模式之间的关系。作者希望通过系统性梳理，揭示q参数在不同AI场景中反复出现的"可在稠密/均匀与稀疏/尖峰行为间插值"的设计模式。

Method: 本文采用综述与结构化分析的方法：先回顾Tsallis统计的数学基础（q-熵变分原理、q-指数/q-对数函数、q-中心极限定理、q-高斯分布及超统计学动力学起源）；随后横跨softmax泛化、强化学习、序列与图神经网络、生成与概率建模、损失设计、优化等领域，提取共性设计模式；最后通过理论论证将深度网络的权重谱和梯度噪声统计特征与q-统计关联起来。

Result: 揭示了AI中q-统计应用的统一模式：通过单一参数q实现稠密/均匀与稀疏/尖峰行为之间的可调插值。作者论证了深度网络中经验观测到的重尾权重谱和梯度噪声统计本身是非广延（nonextensive）特征，将现代学习动态纳入q-统计的范畴。同时指出了方法论陷阱、与信息几何及q-指数族的关系，并提出q应作为可学习归纳偏置的方向。

Conclusion: Tsallis统计为理解AI中的稀疏性、鲁棒性和重尾现象提供了统一的数学框架；作者倡导将q视为可学习的归纳偏置而非固定超参数，并指出信息几何与q-指数族是未来值得深入探索的方向。

Abstract: Tsallis statistics generalizes Boltzmann-Gibbs statistical mechanics through a single real parameter $q$ that controls the weight assigned to rare and frequent events. Originally proposed to describe physical systems with long-range correlations, multifractal geometry, and heavy-tailed fluctuations, the framework has become a recurring ingredient in modern artificial intelligence (AI): it underlies sparse attention mechanisms (\textsc{sparsemax} and $α$-\textsc{entmax}), maximum-entropy reinforcement learning with controllable exploration, robust and heavy-tailed probabilistic models, and a family of generalized loss functions and regularizers. This paper offers a structured perspective on where Tsallis statistics meets AI. We first review the mathematical core: $q$-entropy and its variational (maximum-entropy) foundation, the $q$-exponential and $q$-logarithm, the $q$-central limit theorem, $q$-Gaussian distributions, and their dynamical origin in superstatistics, emphasizing the properties that matter for machine learning. We then survey applications across softmax generalization, reinforcement learning, sequential and graph neural models, generative and probabilistic modeling, loss design, and optimization, extracting the recurring design pattern in each case: a tunable interpolation between dense/uniform and sparse/peaked behavior governed by $q$. We further argue that the heavy-tailed weight spectra and gradient-noise statistics empirically observed in deep networks are themselves nonextensive signatures, placing modern learning dynamics within the scope of $q$-statistics. Finally, we discuss methodological pitfalls, the relationship to information geometry and $q$-exponential families, and open directions, arguing that $q$ should be treated as a learnable inductive bias rather than a fixed hyperparameter.

</details>


### [296] [CT-PrepAgent: Bounded Policy and Controlled Execution for Adaptive CT Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01233)
*Xiaolin Fan,Yue Pei,Yingying Zhang,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-PrepAgent，一种基于大语言模型代理的自适应CT数据预处理方法，通过有界策略和受控确定性执行实现跨数据源和任务的CT数据自适应准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构CT采集方式和多样化的下游任务需求限制了固定数据预处理工作流的可迁移性。现有方法依赖人工设计或数据集特定规则，难以适应采集条件和分析目标的变化。LLM代理虽在医疗工作流自动化方面有前景，但用于自适应CT数据准备的研究尚属空白。

Method: CT-PrepAgent通过三个核心组件实现：确定性检查构建结构化数据-任务画像；基于画像的策略决定合适的DICOM序列或预定义预处理方案；受控执行流程负责守卫、解析、执行和验证决策，启用有界恢复并在必要时安全隔离。

Result: 在三个公开CT分割任务中达到最高宏观平均Dice；在两个私有原始DICOM队列中，验证输出产出率从61.7%提升至70.0%，且在共同验证输出上获得相似的配准指标。受控故障和回放测试验证了有界恢复、安全隔离和无策略回放的能力。

Conclusion: CT-PrepAgent实现了数据-任务自适应的CT预处理决策，在准确性和产出率方面均优于现有方法，同时通过有界恢复和安全隔离机制保证了系统的鲁棒性与可控性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous computed tomography (CT) acquisitions and diverse downstream task requirements limit the transferability of fixed data preparation workflows across data sources and tasks. Existing approaches typically rely on manually designed or dataset-specific rules, making it difficult to accommodate changes in acquisition conditions and analytical objectives without manual intervention. Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown promise for automating medical workflows, yet their potential for adaptive CT data preparation remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose CT-PrepAgent, which enables adaptive CT data preparation through a bounded policy and controlled deterministic execution. Deterministic inspection constructs structured data--task profiles, from which a policy decides an eligible DICOM series or predefined preprocessing profile, while the controlled execution flow guards, resolves, executes, and verifies the decision with bounded recovery when enabled and safe quarantine otherwise. Across three public CT segmentation tasks, CT-PrepAgent derived data-task adaptive preprocessing decisions and achieved the highest macro-average Dice. On two private raw-DICOM cohorts, CT-PrepAgent increased verified output yield from 61.7\% to 70.0\% and yielded similar registration metrics on common verified outputs. Controlled fault and replay tests validate bounded recovery, safe quarantine, and policy-free replay under tested fault and drift settings.

</details>


### [297] [Learning What to Remember and What to Internalize in LLM Self-Evolution via Adaptive Memory-Parameter Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01234)
*Tianyun Ji,Zhenya Huang,Jiayu Liu,Zirui Liu,Yu Su,Hongbin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COVE框架，统一了基于外部记忆（harness-based）和基于参数（parameter-based）两种智能体自进化范式，通过任务感知路由、阶段感知调度和知识优化来协调两种学习通道，在动态环境中实现更鲁棒和高效的自进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体自进化方法分为两条路线：基于外部化反馈的harness方法（灵活但性能有限）和基于参数内化的方法（能力提升更深但适应性差）。单一机制存在灵活性与性能之间的权衡，缺乏统一协调两种通道的框架。

Method: 提出COVE统一框架，包含三个核心设计：1）任务感知路由——根据任务类型匹配合适的学习机制；2）阶段感知调度——按进化阶段动态安排学习策略；3）知识优化——优化知识匹配与利用。将自进化视为协调任务与知识类型到适当学习机制的匹配过程。

Result: 在多个任务类别的实验中，COVE优于单一通道的进化策略，在变化的環境下表现出更稳健和高效的改进。

Conclusion: 协调harness-based和parameter-based两种学习通道能够突破单一机制的权衡限制，实现更鲁棒的自进化，为智能体在部署后动态环境中持续适应提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly operate in dynamic environments where tool interfaces, APIs, and user requirements change after deployment. Existing self-evolution methods mainly follow two paradigms: harness-based approaches, which externalize feedback into editable memories or skills for rapid adaptation, and parameter-based approaches, which internalize experience into model parameters for deeper capability improvement. However, using either mechanism alone creates a trade-off between flexibility and performance. This paper asks how an agent can coordinate both channels to achieve robust self-evolution. We present COVE, a unified agent self-evolution framework that combines harness-based and parameter-based learning through task-aware routing, stage-aware scheduling, and knowledge optimization. Through this design, COVE treats self-evolution not as indiscriminate accumulation of experience, but as a coordinated process that matches tasks and knowledge types to appropriate learning mechanisms. Experiments across multiple task categories show that COVE outperforms single-channel evolution strategies, demonstrating more robust and efficient improvement under changing environments.

</details>


### [298] [Cognitive Demand Steering for Adaptive Meta-Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01319)
*John Scoville,Shengzhuang Chen,Yejin Bang,Stefan Winzeck,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了训练免费的元推理框架CDS，通过残差需求评估（前向展望而非后向奖励）和16维认知尺度驱动的干预选择，在三个前沿LLM和六个推理基准上平均提升准确率21.9%（对比直接调用）和9%（对比标准思维链）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元推理框架存在三个局限：依赖向后看的奖励函数、使用粗粒度搜索动作、需要额外的推理控制器训练（需要大量少次监督）。这些限制了元推理方法的泛化性和实用性，因此需要一种无需训练、能跨模型跨任务零样本迁移的元推理框架。

Method: CDS框架包含两部分核心设计：（1）残差需求评估：LLM-based进度评估器每个步骤描述完成解答所需的残余推理量，而非仅评估前一步骤质量，提供前瞻性需求信号；（2）认知尺度驱动的干预选择：基于认知科学的16个维度（如注意力与扫描、学习与抽象、空间-物理推理等）来设计干预策略（包括通用示例和动作），并为初始问题复杂度和残差需求信号进行画像，使元控制器拥有细粒度诊断词汇。整体无需任何训练组件，实现零样本跨模型跨任务迁移。

Result: 在三个前沿LLM和六个推理基准上的平均测试中，CDS相比直接调用提升准确率21.9%，相比标准思维链推理提升9%，其中在困难的数学和编程任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: CDS通过前瞻性残差需求评估替代后向奖励、利用认知科学维度的细粒度干预设计，消除了训练需求并实现了强泛化的零样本跨模型迁移，为元推理领域提供了一条无需额外训练成本的训练免费路径。

Abstract: Recent meta-reasoning frameworks improve LLM reasoning by wrapping chain-of-thought generation in an iterative control loop, allowing more effective backtracking, termination of reasoning loops, and injection of promising reasoning patterns, among other strategy adjustments. Despite promising results, methods often rely on backward-looking reward functions, utilize coarse search actions, or require additional reasoning controller training requiring many-shot supervision. We introduce Cognitive Demand Steering (CDS), a training-free meta-reasoning framework equipped with residual demand assessment: at each step, an LLM-based progress evaluator characterizes the residual reasoning required to arrive at a solution rather than merely evaluating the previous step. This allows a meta-controller to select reasoning interventions comprising both general-purpose exemplars and actions (e.g., general guidance for quantitative reasoning) that directly tackle this forward-looking demand signal. This shift eliminates the need for any trained component while enabling zero-shot transfer across models and tasks with no adaptation. Rather than relying on coarse characterizations, we employ cognitive scales to both design interventions as well as profile initial problem complexity and residual demand signal over 16 dimensions motivated by cognitive science (e.g., attention and scan, learning and abstraction, spatio-physical reasoning), giving the controller a fine-grained vocabulary for diagnosing. Averaged across three frontier LLMs and six reasoning benchmarks, CDS improves accuracy by $21.9\%$ over direct calls and $9\%$ over standard CoT reasoning, with the largest gains on difficult mathematics and coding tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [EmergencyBias: Bias in Text-to-Image Models under Emergency Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00598)
*Haibo Tang,Linqi Zhang,Hongxin Huan,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文定义了T2I生成模型在紧急场景下的"EmergencyBias"偏见，涵盖人口统计偏见和行为偏见，并通过七个主流T2I模型、六种紧急场景和三个维度进行系统评估，提出轻量级提示嵌入校准方法ActionAlign来减少行为偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I偏见研究主要关注静态和显式的偏见（如性别、种族、地理文化的代表性差异），而忽略了行为层面的偏见——即不同群体如何被描绘为行动、反应和占据社会角色。紧急场景为研究此类偏见提供了理想的测试环境，因为它要求模型不仅展现谁在场，还要展现谁处于风险中、谁进行干预以及责任如何分配。

Method: 作者定义了EmergencyBias概念（涵盖人口统计偏见和行为偏见），构建了系统化评估框架，在七个领先的T2I模型、六种代表性紧急场景和三个入口统计维度上进行实验评估。同时提出了ActionAlign，一种轻量级的提示嵌入校准方法，用于减少行为差异。

Result: 实验发现三个主要结果：(1) 在无人口统计说明的空提示下，T2I模型在紧急场景中表现出明显的人口统计偏见，体现在性别、年龄和肤色分布上；(2) 在受控提示下，紧急响应中的行为偏见与入口统计变量系统性相关，性别差异尤为显著且各模型间差异大；(3) ActionAlign在减少行为差异方面优于基于提示的基线方法，同时保持图像质量。

Conclusion: 紧急场景是T2I模型偏见评估的重要场景，本研究为公平视觉生成提供了实际可行的方向，特别是在对社会影响重大的情境下。

Abstract: Bias in Text-to-Image (T2I) generation has become an important problem in multimedia content creation and communication. However, existing studies have primarily focused on relatively static and explicit forms of bias, such as disparities in the representation of gender, race, and geo-cultural attributes. Less attention has been paid to behavioral bias in how different groups are portrayed acting, reacting, and occupying social roles. Emergency scenarios provide a revealing setting for studying such bias because they require models to depict not only who is present, but also who is at risk, who intervenes, and how responsibility is allocated. In this paper, we define EmergencyBias, a form of bias in T2I generation under emergency scenarios that includes both demographic bias and behavioral bias. We construct an evaluation framework to systematically study EmergencyBias across seven leading T2I models, six representative emergency scenarios, and three demographic dimensions. Our experimental results reveal three main findings. First, under blank prompts without demographic specification, T2I models exhibit clear demographic bias in emergency scenarios, reflected in the distributions of portrayed individuals across gender, age, and skin tone. Second, under controlled prompts, behavioral bias in emergency responses remains systematically associated with demographic variation, with particularly pronounced disparities along gender and substantial differences across models. Third, we introduce ActionAlign, a lightweight prompt-embedding calibration method that outperforms a representative prompt-based baseline in reducing behavioral disparities while largely preserving image quality. Overall, our work identifies emergency scenarios as an important setting for bias evaluation in T2I models and offers a practical direction toward fairer visual generation in socially consequential contexts.

</details>


### [300] [FATE: Frame-Level Audio-Visual Temporal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01310)
*Kaisi Guan,Bingzi Zhang,Xihua Wang,Ying Ba,Xin Cheng,Yijing Chen,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FATE（帧级音视频时间嵌入）模型，通过保留帧级序列而非池化为单一嵌入，结合跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时间对比学习的联合目标，同时捕获音视频的语义与时间对齐信息，在时间与语义检索、事件定位和生成评估等任务上均超越现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频模型无法同时兼顾语义和时间对齐：嵌入模型能匹配语义但丢失时间信息，同步模型能捕获时间偏移但缺乏语义理解，二者各有不足。

Method: FATE保留每一模态的帧级序列并在物理时间线上对齐，计算严格对齐帧对之间的相似度；采用联合训练目标，结合跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时间对比学习，将同步信息编码进可复用的嵌入空间。

Result: 在三个任务上，FATE在时间与语义检索上大幅超越最强基线；在零样本事件定位上匹敌全监督方法；作为生成评估指标时与人类判断相关性最高。

Conclusion: FATE证明了帧级保留与时间对齐的结合可以有效弥合语义与时间两个维度之间的鸿沟，为音视频理解提供了统一且可复用的嵌入表示方案。

Abstract: When a dog opens its mouth and barks, humans naturally recognize what the sound is and when it occurs. Building audio-visual models with this same ability requires representations that capture both semantic and temporal alignment. Current approaches fall short on one side or the other: embedding models match semantic but lose temporal information; synchronization models capture temporal offsets but lack semantic understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose FATE, Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding. Unlike prior embedding models that pool each modality into a single embedding and discard temporal information, FATE retains frame-level sequences, aligns them on the physical timeline, and computes similarity over strictly aligned frame pairs. Unlike synchronization models that output only an offset prediction, FATE encodes synchronization in a reusable embedding space, trained with a joint objective combining cross-video semantic and within-video temporal contrastive learning to capture both what sounds and when it occurs. Across three tasks, FATE surpasses the strongest baseline on temporal and semantic retrieval by a large margin, matches fully supervised methods on event localization in a zero-shot setting, and achieves the best correlation with human judgments as a generation evaluation metric. The source code can be found at \texttt{https://github.com/guankaisi/FATE}.

</details>


### [301] [Hear, Invoke, and Understand: A Skill-Calling Multimodal Agent for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01881)
*Yuwen Wang,Tian-Hao Zhang,Minghao Cai,Yilin Ren,Ziyang Jiang,Xin Wang,Zhichao Wang,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan,Xinyuan Qian*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechAgent-R，一个能够与外部工具交互进行音频推理的音频智能体，并构建了包含65,492条交互轨迹和507.6小时音频的HIU-Corpus数据集。通过轨迹监督微调和多轮强化学习训练，系统在ID和OOD任务上分别达到84.17和70.94分，相比基础模型提升15.40和14.23分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂声学问题需要模型执行声学操作、与外部工具交互并基于文本或处理后的音频观察进行推理，而非直接从固定音频输入给出答案。现有研究缺乏对工具交互式音频推理问题的系统性研究。

Method: SpeechAgent-R采用两阶段训练策略：首先通过轨迹级监督微调学习结构化交互行为，然后通过多轮强化学习提升决策能力。同时构建了HIU-Corpus数据集（24个任务、8项技能、9种工具）和HIU-Bench评估基准（56个任务，含ID和OOD划分）。

Result: SpeechAgent-R在ID任务上达到84.17分，在OOD任务上达到70.94分，相比同一智能体框架下的基础模型分别提升15.40和14.23分。

Conclusion: 学习技能和工具的协调配合能够显著提升音频智能体处理多样化任务场景和自适应工具交互的能力。

Abstract: Complex acoustic problems may require models to perform acoustic operations, interact with external tools and reason over the resulting textual or processed-audio observations rather than answer directly from a fixed audio input. We study such problems as tool-interactive audio reasoning and develop SpeechAgent-R, an audio agent that coordinates its intrinsic multimodal understanding with external skills and tools. To support this capability, we construct HIU-Corpus, comprising 65,492 interaction trajectories and 507.6 hours of audio across 24 tasks, 8 skills and 9 tools. SpeechAgent-R first learns structured interaction behaviors through trajectory-based supervised fine-tuning and then improves its decisions through multi-turn reinforcement learning. We further introduce HIU-Bench to jointly evaluate task performance, interaction quality and generalization to diverse task settings. It contains 1,395 samples across 56 tasks, including in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) splits with substantial shifts in tool usage and workflow composition. SpeechAgent-R achieves 84.17 on ID tasks and 70.94 on OOD tasks, improving over the base model under the same agent harness by 15.40 and 14.23 points. These results demonstrate that learning skill and tool coordination improves audio agents' ability to handle diverse task settings and adaptive tool interactions.

</details>


### [302] [AcoustiTrace: When Plausible Sound Violates Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02035)
*Shiyang Li,Yuewen Cao,Yihao Liu,Yuandong Pu,Baochang Zhang,Xiaofei Li,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AcoustiTrace，一个用于诊断音视频生成中声学物理真实性的基准测试，围绕声学过程组织评估，包含八个基于可测量声学量的维度，并构建了大规模数据集和提示套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成器虽然能产生语义合理且表面同步的声音，但仍可能违反可见事件和环境所隐含的声学物理过程。现有基准无法将这些违规定位到具体的声学过程并量化其严重程度。

Method: AcoustiTrace围绕声学过程组织T2AV和I2AV评估，涵盖声音生成、传播环境和声学接收三个方面，通过八个基于可测量声学量的维度进行评估；构建大规模声学机制数据集（含真实音视频记录和声学标注的RGB-D观测），开发针对性提示套件和验证过的评估器。

Result: 实验表明即使领先的生成器在产生合理声音事件的同时，仍难以处理基本的声学过程。此外，AcoustiTrace提供的声学关系诊断可用于指导模型改进。

Conclusion: AcoustiTrace的诊断能力可以为模型精炼提供指导，并开辟将声学原理纳入训练目标、奖励建模和候选选择的新方向，推动更物理保真的音视频生成。

Abstract: Recent audio-video generators can produce semantically plausible and apparently synchronized sound, yet may still violate the acoustic processes implied by visible events and environments. Existing benchmarks provide limited support for attributing such violations to particular acoustic processes and quantifying their severity. We introduce AcoustiTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that formalizes acoustic physical realism in audio-video generation. AcoustiTrace organizes text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) evaluation around the acoustic process, covering sound generation, propagation environment, and acoustic reception through eight dimensions grounded in measurable acoustic quantities. Based on these evaluation dimensions, we construct a large-scale dataset organized around acoustic mechanisms, comprising real-world audio-video recordings and acoustically annotated RGB-D observations, and use it to develop targeted prompt suites and validated evaluators. Experiments reveal that even leading generators still struggle with fundamental acoustic processes despite producing plausible sound events. Finally, we show that the diagnostics AcoustiTrace provides for specific acoustic relations can guide model refinement toward more physically faithful audio and open new directions for incorporating acoustic principles into training objectives, reward modeling, and candidate selection.

</details>


### [303] [Estimating SSIM from MSE for DCT-Based Compressed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02549)
*Luc Trudeau,Maria G. Martini*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出在DCT压缩图像中，仅利用参考图像的局部统计信息（方差或标准差加权）将全局PSNR/MSE重新分配为局部MSE，从而近似SSIM，无需获得真实局部MSE。实验表明该方法在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上优于全局MSE基线，并可自然扩展到视频场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效且感知上有意义的图像质量评估是图像/视频处理、压缩和流媒体系统的基本需求。现有SSIM计算需要局部MSE信息，但实际中往往只能获得全局PSNR或MSE，因此需要一种仅依赖参考图像统计信息来近似SSIM的方法。

Method: 提出两种方法：通过参考图像的方差或标准差加权来重新分配全局MSE为局部MSE，然后基于这些近似的局部MSE计算SSIM。该方法仅需参考图像的局部统计信息，无需访问真实局部MSE。

Result: 在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上，跨多个JPEG质量级别进行实验，两种方法均提供了准确且稳健的SSIM近似，显著优于全局MSE基线。

Conclusion: 该框架可自然扩展到视频领域，其中参考图像推导的统计信息可以在同一内容的多次编码中分摊计算成本，为高效质量评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: Efficient and perceptually meaningful quality assessment is a fundamental requirement for image and video processing, compression, and streaming systems. This article shows that, in the context of Discrete Cosine Transform ( DCT)-based compressed images, Structural Similarity Index ( SSIM ) can be approximated from global Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) or Mean Square Error ( MSE) using local statistics derived only from the reference image. While prior work assumes access to local MSE, we propose two approaches to approximate local MSE by redistributing the global MSE using variance or standard-deviation-based weighting. Experiments on the Kodak and Xiph Subset1 datasets across a range of JPEG quality levels demonstrate that both approaches provide accurate and robust SSIM approximations, substantially outperforming the global MSE baseline. The proposed framework is designed to extend naturally to video, where reference-derived statistics can be amortized across multiple encodes of the same content.

</details>


### [304] [A Brief Overview about D-Profile of Ginga DTV Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02565)
*Marcelo F. Moreno,Debora C. Muchaluat-Saade,Guido Lemos de Souza Filho,Raoni Kulesza,Alan L. V. Guedes*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西数字电视系统标准的最新修订，重点聚焦于Ginga中间件D-Profile接收器规范所推动的广播与宽带服务深度融合的新用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西DTV系统标准需要更新以支持广播与宽带服务深度融合的新用例，同时不能破坏现有DTV服务，因为巴西及许多ISDB-T国家仍处于模拟向数字切换的过程中。

Method: 通过中间件层（Ginga）作为开放平台，基于接收器配置文件（profiles）进行增量式演进，由巴西DTV论坛在学术界主导的协作努力下制定D-Profile Ginga DTV接收器规范。

Result: Ginga作为巴西DTV中间件，通过D-Profile接收器规范实现了广播与宽带服务的深度融合，在保持现有服务不中断的情况下取得了令人瞩目的成果。

Conclusion: 中间件层是DTV系统实现增量式（且强大）演进的最佳候选方案，Ginga的D-Profile规范是巴西DTV论坛学术协作的成功典范。

Abstract: The Brazilian DTV system standards have been recently revised in order to address new use cases related to deeper integration be- tween broadcast services and broadband services. Such an evolution could not disrupt the current DTV services since Brazil and many ISDB-T countries are still under the switch-off process from ana- log to digital. The middleware layer is the best candidate for such an incremental (yet powerful) evolution. In the case of Ginga, the Brazilian DTV middleware, as an open platform based on receiver profiles, the results were impressive. In this paper, we focus on the advances and new use cases addressed by the D-profile Ginga DTV receiver specification carried out in the Brazilian DTV Forum under a collaborative effort led by the Academia

</details>
